 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""rumpun audit — reflection over the season ledger (DESIGN section 15).
     2	
     3	Phase-2 self-evolution needs reflection: run_audit reads the last N musim
     4	seasons, records which ledger artifacts each engine season (a season with a
     5	rimba/<sid>/ dir) actually wrote, and appends one akar record of
     6	deterministic findings plus at most three candidate mutations — every
     7	findings and candidate line cites the season ids it rests on. The verb
     8	mutates nothing except appending that record: candidates reach the pipeline
     9	only through the existing evolve gate (lint plus operator at the manual
    10	stage).
    11	
    12	Deterministic trigger rules — nothing outside these may propose a mutation:
    13	- a declared phase whose `writes` artifact exists in 0 of >= 2 engine
    14	  seasons -> "exercise or trim phase P" (pipeline order, cap applies)
    15	- a route with >= 2 clean-empty agent spawns -> "add spawn tool-check or
    16	  prefer alternative route" (after the phase triggers, cap applies)
    17	- >= 2 LOSS seasons that each shipped >= 1 integrated module ->
    18	  "recalibrate LOSS bands" (after the route triggers, cap applies)
    19	- stopped_stall in >= 2 audited seasons -> "re-size stall/budget rules"
    20	  (last among the candidates)
    21	The budget cap never proposes a mutation: campaign_cost_cap unset is a
    22	finding only — the operator sets the number, the tool just flags it.
    23	
    24	yamlio.YamlError from a season file or rumpun.yaml propagates unwrapped
    25	(the CLI already reports it); akar.AkarError wraps into AuditError.
    26	"""
    27	
    28	from __future__ import annotations
    29	
    30	import json
    31	import logging
    32	import re
    33	from collections import Counter
    34	from pathlib import Path
    35	from typing import Any
    36	
    37	from rumpun import akar, yamlio
    38	
    39	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    40	
    41	LEDGER_ARTIFACTS = (
    42	    "errors.jsonl",
    43	    "gaps.yaml",
    44	    "hypotheses.yaml",
    45	    "experiments.yaml",
    46	    "falsification.yaml",
    47	    "results.jsonl",
    48	    "verdicts.jsonl",
    49	    "report.html",
    50	)
    51	"""Ledger artifacts checked per engine season at rimba/<sid>/."""
    52	
    53	HISTOGRAM_KEYS = ("WIN", "LOSS", "INVALID")
    54	STALL_RECURRENCE = 2
    55	ROUTED_EMPTY_TRIGGER = 2
    56	BAND_MASKED_SEASONS = 2
    57	MAX_CANDIDATES = 3
    58	
    59	BOOKKEEPING_FILES = frozenset(
    60	    {
    61	        "agent.log",
    62	        "exit",
    63	        "prompt.md",
    64	        "prompt-meta.yaml",
    65	        "state.json",
    66	        "terminated",
    67	        "terminated.tmp",
    68	        "__pycache__",
    69	    }
    70	)
    71	"""Engine-owned entries of an agent workspace; never a deliverable.
    72	
    73	A file counts as bookkeeping when any component of its workspace-relative
    74	path is in this set, so __pycache__/*.pyc stays bookkeeping too.
    75	"""
    76	
    77	OUTCOME_KEYS = ("clean-deliverable", "clean-empty", "failed", "not-exited")
    78	"""Spawn outcome categories, record order for the F4 per-route line."""
    79	
    80	_SEASON_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"^s(\d+)\.yaml$")
    81	_AUDIT_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}_audit-\d+\.md$")
    82	
    83	
    84	class AuditError(Exception):
    85	    """Invalid audit input or unreadable ledger state."""
    86	
    87	
    88	def _seasons(root: Path, last_n: int) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    89	    """(sid, yaml path) for the last N musim seasons, sorted by number.
    90	
    91	    sid follows the season-list convention: the yaml id field, stem fallback.
    92	    """
    93	    musim = root / "musim"
    94	    if not musim.is_dir():
    95	        raise AuditError(f"no musim directory under {root}: nothing to audit")
    96	    numbered = sorted(
    97	        (int(m.group(1)), p)
    98	        for p in musim.iterdir()
    99	        if p.is_file() and (m := _SEASON_FILE_RE.match(p.name))
   100	    )
   101	    if not numbered:
   102	        raise AuditError(f"no musim/s<N>.yaml seasons under {musim}: nothing to audit")
   103	    window: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []
   104	    for _num, path in numbered[-last_n:]:
   105	        doc = yamlio.load(path)
   106	        sid = doc.get("id") if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   107	        window.append((str(sid) if sid else path.stem, path))
   108	    return window
   109	
   110	
   111	def _declared_phases(latest: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   112	    """(phase, writes) pairs of the latest season yaml's methodology.pipeline.
   113	
   114	    A pipeline entry without a string phase and a string writes carries no
   115	    liveness evidence to check; it is skipped with a warning, never silently.
   116	    """
   117	    doc = yamlio.load(latest)
   118	    method = doc.get("methodology") if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   119	    pipeline = (method or {}).get("pipeline") or []
   120	    phases: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   121	    for entry in pipeline:
   122	        if (
   123	            isinstance(entry, dict)
   124	            and isinstance(entry.get("phase"), str)
   125	            and isinstance(entry.get("writes"), str)
   126	        ):
   127	            phases.append((entry["phase"], entry["writes"]))
   128	        else:
   129	            logger.warning("skipping pipeline entry without phase/writes in %s", latest)
   130	    return phases
   131	
   132	
   133	def _season_artifacts(root: Path, sid: str) -> frozenset[str]:
   134	    """Which ledger artifacts exist at rimba/<sid>/ (empty when no dir)."""
   135	    sdir = root / "rimba" / sid
   136	    if not sdir.is_dir():
   137	        return frozenset()
   138	    return frozenset(name for name in LEDGER_ARTIFACTS if (sdir / name).is_file())
   139	
   140	
   141	def _season_state(root: Path, sid: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   142	    """The season's parsed _season/state.json; None when absent or not a dict.
   143	
   144	    One read feeds both the F2 status rule and the F4 agent snapshots; a
   145	    corrupt state refuses the audit rather than guessing.
   146	    """
   147	    path = root / "rimba" / sid / "_season" / "state.json"
   148	    if not path.is_file():
   149	        return None
   150	    try:
   151	        state = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   152	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   153	        logger.exception("cannot read season state %s", path)
   154	        raise AuditError(f"cannot read {path}: {exc}") from exc
   155	    return state if isinstance(state, dict) else None
   156	
   157	
   158	def _read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
   159	    """Parsed JSONL rows of one ledger file; [] when the file is absent.
   160	
   161	    A row without the keys a caller looks for is not a statement and does
   162	    not count toward that rule (a defined rule, not a silent drop). A
   163	    corrupt line refuses the audit rather than guessing.
   164	    """
   165	    if not path.is_file():
   166	        return []
   167	    rows: list[dict] = []
   168	    try:
   169	        for lineno, line in enumerate(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
   170	            if not line.strip():
   171	                continue
   172	            try:
   173	                rows.append(json.loads(line))
   174	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   175	                raise AuditError(f"{path}: line {lineno} is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc
   176	    except OSError as exc:
   177	        logger.exception("cannot read %s", path)
   178	        raise AuditError(f"cannot read {path}: {exc}") from exc
   179	    return rows
   180	
   181	
   182	def _verdict_rows(root: Path, sid: str) -> list[dict]:
   183	    """verdicts.jsonl rows of one season; [] when the file is absent."""
   184	    return _read_jsonl(root / "rimba" / sid / "verdicts.jsonl")
   185	
   186	
   187	def _result_rows(root: Path, sid: str) -> list[dict]:
   188	    """results.jsonl rows of one season; [] when the file is absent."""
   189	    return _read_jsonl(root / "rimba" / sid / "results.jsonl")
   190	
   191	
   192	def _season_verdict(rows: list[dict], sid: str) -> str | None:
   193	    """Verdict of the last season-level verdicts row for sid; None if absent.
   194	
   195	    Season-level follows the report verb's rule [H]: a row whose `season`
   196	    value is this sid; the last such row wins.
   197	    """
   198	    verdict = ""
   199	    for row in rows:
   200	        if row.get("season") == sid:
   201	            verdict = str(row.get("verdict", ""))
   202	    return verdict or None
   203	
   204	
   205	def _has_deliverable(ws: Path) -> bool:
   206	    """Any file under the agent workspace the engine does not own (recursive).
   207	
   208	    A file is bookkeeping when any component of its workspace-relative path
   209	    is in BOOKKEEPING_FILES; everything else is a deliverable.
   210	    """
   211	    if not ws.is_dir():
   212	        return False
   213	    for path in ws.rglob("*"):
   214	        if not path.is_file():
   215	            continue
   216	        parts = path.relative_to(ws).parts
   217	        if not any(part in BOOKKEEPING_FILES for part in parts):
   218	            return True
   219	    return False
   220	
   221	
   222	def _spawn_outcome(snap: dict, deliverable: bool) -> str:
   223	    """Classify one finalized agent snap into an OUTCOME_KEYS category.
   224	
   225	    exit_code 0 splits on deliverable presence (clean-empty is the tool-less
   226	    signature); a non-zero exit or an unparseable exit file (engine state
   227	    "failed") is failed; anything that never reached exited/failed —
   228	    terminated, crashed, stalled, running — is not-exited. An exited snap
   229	    always carries a parsed exit_code (engine contract), so code None with
   230	    state exited cannot occur; it classifies not-exited rather than guessing.
   231	    """
   232	    code = snap.get("exit_code")
   233	    if code == 0:
   234	        return "clean-deliverable" if deliverable else "clean-empty"
   235	    if code is not None or snap.get("state") == "failed":
   236	        return "failed"
   237	    return "not-exited"
   238	
   239	
   240	def _budget_cap(root: Path) -> Any:
   241	    """The campaign cost cap from rumpun.yaml; None when unset or absent.
   242	
   243	    The key lives at budget.campaign_cost_cap — the top-level `budget:` block
   244	    scaffold.py writes into the campaign config. A set value is never
   245	    interpreted here, only its presence is flagged.
   246	    """
   247	    path = root / "rumpun.yaml"
   248	    if not path.is_file():
   249	        return None
   250	    doc = yamlio.load(path)
   251	    budget = doc.get("budget") if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   252	    if not isinstance(budget, dict):
   253	        return None
   254	    return budget.get("campaign_cost_cap")
   255	
   256	
   257	def _next_index(root: Path) -> int:
   258	    """1 + count of existing audit-* record files in akar/, by filename scan."""
   259	    akar_dir = root / "akar"
   260	    if not akar_dir.is_dir():
   261	        return 1
   262	    return 1 + sum(
   263	        1 for entry in akar_dir.iterdir() if entry.is_file() and _AUDIT_FILE_RE.match(entry.name)
   264	    )
   265	
   266	
   267	def _findings_and_candidates(
   268	    root: Path,
   269	    seasons: list[tuple[str, Path]],
   270	) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   271	    """Deterministic, season-cited body lines: findings, then candidates.
   272	
   273	    Split out of run_audit so every rule is testable without touching akar.
   274	    """
   275	    latest_sid, latest_yaml = seasons[-1]
   276	    engine_ids = [
   277	        sid for sid, _path in seasons if (root / "rimba" / sid).is_dir()
   278	    ]
   279	    n = len(engine_ids)
   280	    ids_str = ",".join(engine_ids)
   281	
   282	    phases = _declared_phases(latest_yaml)
   283	    artifacts = {sid: _season_artifacts(root, sid) for sid in engine_ids}
   284	
   285	    lines: list[str] = [
   286	        f"scope: last {len(seasons)} musim seasons "
   287	        f"({','.join(sid for sid, _p in seasons)}); engine seasons with rimba/ "
   288	        f"({n}): {ids_str}",
   289	        "declared phases from latest season "
   290	        + latest_sid
   291	        + ": "
   292	        + (", ".join(f"{phase}->{writes}" for phase, writes in phases) or "none"),
   293	    ]
   294	
   295	    # F1 phase liveness: K of N engine seasons hold each declared phase's
   296	    # writes artifact. Liveness 0 across the window is unexercised-phase
   297	    # evidence; partial liveness is evidence the phase does run sometimes.
   298	    liveness: list[tuple[str, str, int]] = []
   299	    for phase, writes in phases:
   300	        k = sum(1 for sid in engine_ids if writes in artifacts[sid])
   301	        liveness.append((phase, writes, k))
   302	        lines.append(
   303	            f"F1 phase liveness: phase {phase} (writes {writes}) wrote its "
   304	            f"artifact in {k} of {n} engine seasons ({ids_str})"
   305	        )
   306	
   307	    # F2 stall recurrence: harness-observed stopped_stall terminal states.
   308	    states = {sid: _season_state(root, sid) for sid in engine_ids}
   309	    with_state = [sid for sid, s in states.items() if s is not None]
   310	    stalled = [sid for sid, s in states.items() if (s or {}).get("status") == "stopped_stall"]
   311	    if with_state:
   312	        lines.append(
   313	            f"F2 stall recurrence: stopped_stall in {len(stalled)} of "
   314	            f"{len(with_state)} seasons with state.json "
   315	            f"({','.join(stalled) if stalled else 'none'}); "
   316	            f"recurrence {'yes' if len(stalled) >= STALL_RECURRENCE else 'no'} "
   317	            f"(threshold {STALL_RECURRENCE})"
   318	        )
   319	    else:
   320	        lines.append(f"F2 stall recurrence: no engine season has a state.json ({ids_str})")
   321	
   322	    # F3 verdict histogram over every window season's verdicts.jsonl.
   323	    hist: Counter[str] = Counter()
   324	    sources: list[str] = []
   325	    bare: list[str] = []
   326	    verdict_rows: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   327	    for sid in engine_ids:
   328	        rows = _verdict_rows(root, sid)
   329	        verdict_rows[sid] = rows
   330	        if rows:
   331	            sources.append(sid)
   332	        else:
   333	            bare.append(sid)
   334	        for row in rows:
   335	            verdict = row.get("verdict")
   336	            if isinstance(verdict, str):
   337	                hist[verdict] += 1
   338	    other = sum(hist.values()) - sum(hist[key] for key in HISTOGRAM_KEYS)
   339	    lines.append(
   340	        "F3 verdict histogram over verdicts.jsonl of "
   341	        f"{','.join(sources) if sources else 'none'}"
   342	        + (f" (none in: {','.join(bare)})" if bare else "")
   343	        + ": "
   344	        + ", ".join(f"{key} {hist[key]}" for key in HISTOGRAM_KEYS)
   345	        + (f", other {other}" if other else "")
   346	    )
   347	
   348	    # F4 route outcomes: classify every finalized agent snap (engine writes
   349	    # agents{name: snap} into _season/state.json at finalize) per route.
   350	    # A season still running has no agents block yet and contributes nothing.
   351	    route_counts: dict[str, Counter[str]] = {}
   352	    route_sids: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   353	    routed = False
   354	    for sid in engine_ids:
   355	        agents = (states[sid] or {}).get("agents")
   356	        if not isinstance(agents, dict) or not agents:
   357	            continue
   358	        routed = True
   359	        for name in sorted(agents):
   360	            snap = agents[name]
   361	            if not isinstance(snap, dict):
   362	                logger.warning("skipping non-dict agent snap %s in season %s", name, sid)
   363	                continue
   364	            route = str(snap.get("route", "?"))
   365	            deliverable = _has_deliverable(root / "rimba" / sid / name)
   366	            route_counts.setdefault(route, Counter())[_spawn_outcome(snap, deliverable)] += 1
   367	            sids = route_sids.setdefault(route, [])
   368	            if sid not in sids:
   369	                sids.append(sid)
   370	    if not routed:
   371	        lines.append(
   372	            f"F4 route outcomes: no finalized agent snapshots in audited engine "
   373	            f"seasons ({ids_str})"
   374	        )
   375	    for route in sorted(route_counts):
   376	        counts = route_counts[route]
   377	        lines.append(
   378	            f"F4 route outcomes: route {route}: "
   379	            + ", ".join(f"{counts[key]} {key}" for key in OUTCOME_KEYS)
   380	            + f" over {sum(counts.values())} spawns ({','.join(route_sids[route])})"
   381	        )
   382	
   383	    # F5 band calibration: a LOSS season that still shipped integrated
   384	    # modules is a band that masked delivered value (s4/s5/s6 precedent).
   385	    harvested = [
   386	        sid for sid in engine_ids if (root / "rimba" / sid / "results.jsonl").is_file()
   387	    ]
   388	    masked: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
   389	    for sid in harvested:
   390	        integrated = sum(1 for row in _result_rows(root, sid) if row.get("integrated") is True)
   391	        if _season_verdict(verdict_rows.get(sid, []), sid) == "LOSS" and integrated >= 1:
   392	            masked.append((sid, integrated))
   393	            lines.append(
   394	                f"F5 band calibration: LOSS season {sid} shipped {integrated} "
   395	                f"integrated module(s) — band masked value"
   396	            )
   397	    if not masked:
   398	        scope = (
   399	            ",".join(harvested)
   400	            if harvested
   401	            else f"no engine season holds results.jsonl ({ids_str})"
   402	        )
   403	        lines.append(f"F5 band calibration: no LOSS season shipped integrated modules ({scope})")
   404	
   405	    # F6 budget compliance: a flag, never a candidate. The operator sets the
   406	    # number; the audit only reports that it is still unset (P9).
   407	    if _budget_cap(root) is None:
   408	        lines.append(
   409	            "F6 budget compliance: campaign_cost_cap unset since campaign start (P9) "
   410	            "— operator sets the number; the tool only flags"
   411	        )
   412	
   413	    # Candidates: deterministic triggers only, phases in pipeline order
   414	    # first, then route, then calibration, stall trigger last, hard cap.
   415	    # Never invented, never over cap.
   416	    proposals: list[tuple[str, str]] = []  # (short label, record line)
   417	    if n >= 2:
   418	        for phase, writes, k in liveness:
   419	            if k == 0:
   420	                proposals.append(
   421	                    (
   422	                        f"{phase} ({writes})",
   423	                        f"candidate: exercise or trim phase {phase} — {writes} "
   424	                        f"written in 0 of {n} engine seasons ({ids_str}); "
   425	                        f"band: WIN when a later season's ledger holds {writes} "
   426	                        f"or the pipeline drops {phase}",
   427	                    )
   428	                )
   429	    for route in sorted(route_counts):
   430	        empty = route_counts[route]["clean-empty"]
   431	        if empty >= ROUTED_EMPTY_TRIGGER:
   432	            proposals.append(
   433	                (
   434	                    f"route {route} ({empty} clean-empty)",
   435	                    f"candidate: route {route}: {empty} clean-empty spawns — add spawn "
   436	                    f"tool-check or prefer alternative route ({','.join(route_sids[route])}); "
   437	                    f"band: WIN when clean-empty rate drops to 0 or a tool-check lands",
   438	                )
   439	            )
   440	    if len(masked) >= BAND_MASKED_SEASONS:
   441	        proposals.append(
   442	            (
   443	                "LOSS band calibration",
   444	                f"candidate: recalibrate LOSS bands — count integrated deliverables in "
   445	                f"the band ({','.join(sid for sid, _k in masked)}); "
   446	                f"band: WIN when no later LOSS season ships an integrated module "
   447	                f"its band ignores",
   448	            )
   449	        )
   450	    if len(stalled) >= STALL_RECURRENCE:
   451	        proposals.append(
   452	            (
   453	                "stall/budget rules",
   454	                f"candidate: re-size stall/budget rules — stopped_stall in "
   455	                f"{len(stalled)} seasons ({','.join(stalled)}); "
   456	                f"band: WIN when a later season reaches a terminal state "
   457	                f"without stopped_stall",
   458	            )
   459	        )
   460	    dropped = [label for label, _line in proposals[MAX_CANDIDATES:]]
   461	    candidates = [line for _label, line in proposals[:MAX_CANDIDATES]]
   462	    if not candidates:
   463	        lines.append("candidates: none — no trigger met; nothing proposed without evidence")
   464	    lines.extend(candidates)
   465	    if dropped:
   466	        lines.append(
   467	            f"candidate cap {MAX_CANDIDATES} reached in pipeline order; "
   468	            f"also triggered but unproposed here: {','.join(dropped)}"
   469	        )
   470	    return lines, candidates
   471	
   472	
   473	def run_audit(root: Path, last_n: int = 10) -> Path:
   474	    """Reflect over the last `last_n` seasons; append the audit akar record.
   475	
   476	    Reads musim/s*.yaml (sorted by number, last N), the ledger artifacts
   477	    under rimba/<sid>/, the latest season's declared pipeline, per-season
   478	    state.json statuses and finalized agent snapshots, verdicts.jsonl and
   479	    results.jsonl rows, and the rumpun.yaml budget block. Every claim the
   480	    record makes cites its season ids; the only mutation is the appended
   481	    record (id audit-<k>, k = 1 + existing audit-* records by akar/
   482	    filename scan). akar.AkarError wraps into AuditError. Returns the
   483	    record path.
   484	    """
   485	    if last_n < 1:
   486	        raise AuditError(f"last_n must be >= 1, got {last_n}")
   487	    seasons = _seasons(root, last_n)
   488	    engine_ids = [sid for sid, _p in seasons if (root / "rimba" / sid).is_dir()]
   489	    if not engine_ids:
   490	        raise AuditError(
   491	            f"no audited season has a rimba/ directory under {root}; "
   492	            "nothing to reflect on"
   493	        )
   494	    body, _candidates = _findings_and_candidates(root, seasons)
   495	    record_id = f"audit-{_next_index(root)}"
   496	    try:
   497	        path = akar.append_record(root, record_id, "reflection audit", "\n".join(body))
   498	    except akar.AkarError as exc:
   499	        raise AuditError(f"cannot append audit record {record_id}: {exc}") from exc
   500	    return path
   501	
   502	
   503	def candidate_lines(record: Path) -> list[str]:
   504	    """The record's candidate lines, for the verb's own output.
   505	
   506	    Body recovery follows the akar layout docstring: body lines are
   507	    lines[4:-1] between the header and the sha256 trailer.
   508	    """
   509	    try:
   510	        lines = record.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
   511	    except OSError as exc:
   512	        logger.exception("cannot read audit record %s", record)
   513	        raise AuditError(f"cannot read {record}: {exc}") from exc
   514	    return [line for line in lines[4:-1] if line.startswith("candidate:")]

## 15. Campaign phase 2 — self-evolution (operator order 2026-09-14)

The operator ordered the season chain to never stop: every season close
auto-seeds the next. Phase 1 (build the tool) is closed at s10. Phase 2 goal
(rumpun.yaml updated accordingly): rumpun evolves itself — each season,
reflection over the akar ledger generates candidate mutations of the
pipeline and parameters; every mutation carries cited evidence, a band, and
a rollback, and applies only through the existing gate (lint, operator at
manual stage). s11 builds the reflection verb (`rumpun audit`); its record
over s2–s10 is the first ledger-driven input to s12's season YAML.

     1	"""akar: append-only record writer for rumpun project state.
     2	
     3	Records live under ``<root>/akar/`` as ``<YYYY-MM-DD>_<record_id>.md``. Each
     4	file is a small header (id, date, title), the body, and a trailing
     5	``sha256:`` line digesting the body bytes — the digest backs P11 evidence
     6	refs of the form ``akar:<record-id>@<sha256>``. Records are immutable once
     7	written; appending is the only mutation this module performs.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	import hashlib
    11	import logging
    12	import os
    13	from datetime import date
    14	from pathlib import Path
    15	
    16	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17	
    18	_ID_PREFIX = "id: "
    19	
    20	
    21	class AkarError(Exception):
    22	    """Invalid record input, record conflict, unreadable akar state, or lookup miss."""
    23	
    24	
    25	def _validate(record_id: str, title: str) -> None:
    26	    """Reject ids/titles that would break the record layout or escape akar/."""
    27	    forbidden = ("/", "\\", "\0", "\n", "\r")
    28	    if not record_id or any(ch in record_id for ch in forbidden):
    29	        raise AkarError(f"invalid record_id {record_id!r}")
    30	    if "\n" in title or "\r" in title:
    31	        raise AkarError(f"title of {record_id!r} must be a single line")
    32	
    33	
    34	def _declared(root: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    35	    """Map every record id declared in akar/ to its file, in sorted filename order."""
    36	    akar = root / "akar"
    37	    if not akar.is_dir():
    38	        return {}
    39	    found: dict[str, Path] = {}
    40	    for path in sorted(akar.iterdir()):
    41	        if not path.is_file():
    42	            continue
    43	        try:
    44	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    45	        except OSError as exc:
    46	            logger.exception("cannot read existing file %s", path)
    47	            raise AkarError(f"cannot read {path}") from exc
    48	        for line in text.splitlines():
    49	            if line.startswith(_ID_PREFIX):
    50	                rid = line.removeprefix(_ID_PREFIX).strip()
    51	                if rid in found:
    52	                    logger.warning(
    53	                        "record id %s declared in both %s and %s", rid, found[rid], path
    54	                    )
    55	                else:
    56	                    found[rid] = path
    57	                break
    58	        else:
    59	            logger.debug("ignoring %s: no id line", path)
    60	    return found
    61	
    62	
    63	def append_record(root: Path, record_id: str, title: str, body: str) -> Path:
    64	    """Append one record and return its path.
    65	
    66	    File layout, one element per line (the body may itself span lines):
    67	
    68	        # akar record: <record_id>
    69	        id: <record_id>
    70	        date: <YYYY-MM-DD>
    71	        title: <title>
    72	        <body>
    73	        sha256: <sha256 hex of the utf-8 body>
    74	
    75	    The body is recoverable exactly as ``"\\n".join(lines[4:-1])``.
    76	
    77	    Raises AkarError when record_id is invalid, already declared by any file
    78	    in akar/, or the target filename already exists. Existing records are
    79	    never modified.
    80	    """
    81	    _validate(record_id, title)
    82	    if (dup := _declared(root).get(record_id)) is not None:
    83	        raise AkarError(f"record_id {record_id!r} already declared in {dup}")
    84	
    85	    iso_date = date.today().isoformat()
    86	    final = root / "akar" / f"{iso_date}_{record_id}.md"
    87	    if final.exists():
    88	        raise AkarError(f"{final} already exists; akar is append-only")
    89	
    90	    digest = hashlib.sha256(body.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    91	    text = (
    92	        "\n".join(
    93	            [
    94	                f"# akar record: {record_id}",
    95	                f"id: {record_id}",
    96	                f"date: {iso_date}",
    97	                f"title: {title}",
    98	                body,
    99	                f"sha256: {digest}",
   100	            ]
   101	        )
   102	        + "\n"
   103	    )
   104	
   105	    tmp = final.with_name(f".{final.name}.tmp")
   106	    try:
   107	        final.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   108	        with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   109	            fh.write(text)
   110	            fh.flush()
   111	            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
   112	        os.replace(tmp, final)
   113	    except OSError as exc:
   114	        logger.exception("cannot append record %s to %s", record_id, final)
   115	        try:
   116	            tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   117	        except OSError:
   118	            logger.exception("cannot remove tmp file %s", tmp)
   119	        raise AkarError(f"cannot append record {record_id!r} to {final}") from exc
   120	    logger.info("appended akar record %s -> %s", record_id, final)
   121	    return final
   122	
   123	
   124	def find_record(root: Path, record_id: str) -> Path:
   125	    """Return the path of the record declaring record_id, or raise AkarError."""
   126	    path = _declared(root).get(record_id)
   127	    if path is None:
   128	        raise AkarError(f"no akar record declares id {record_id!r}")
   129	    return path
     1	"""rumpun harvest — season close into the akar ledger (build order step 4).
     2	
     3	`harvest_season` reads the engine's season snapshot and appends one record per
     4	season close: status, duration, per-agent state table, verdict, implies line.
     5	Manual verdict entry first (build order step 4); the body is derived from
     6	harness-observed state only ([H] per P36), never from agent-authored text.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import logging
    12	import time
    13	from pathlib import Path
    14	from typing import Any
    15	
    16	from rumpun import akar, engine
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	VERDICTS = ("WIN", "LOSS", "NEUTRAL", "INVALID")
    21	
    22	
    23	def _dash(value: Any) -> str:
    24	    """Markdown cell: None renders as '-', anything else as str()."""
    25	    return "-" if value is None else str(value)
    26	
    27	
    28	def _render_body(state: dict[str, Any], verdict: str, implies: str) -> str:
    29	    """Record body: season status, duration, agent table, verdict, implies."""
    30	    started = float(state["started_at"])
    31	    ended = state.get("ended_at") or time.time()
    32	    agents = state.get("agents") or {}
    33	    lines = [
    34	        f"season {state['id']}: {state['status']}",
    35	        f"duration: {ended - started:.0f}s",
    36	        "",
    37	        "| agent | route | state | exit_code | seconds |",
    38	        "|---|---|---|---|---|",
    39	    ]
    40	    for name in sorted(agents):
    41	        a = agents[name]
    42	        lines.append(
    43	            f"| {_dash(a.get('name', name))} | {_dash(a.get('route'))}"
    44	            f" | {_dash(a.get('state'))} | {_dash(a.get('exit_code'))}"
    45	            f" | {_dash(a.get('seconds'))} |"
    46	        )
    47	    lines += ["", f"verdict: {verdict}", f"implies: {implies}"]
    48	    return "\n".join(lines)
    49	
    50	
    51	def harvest_season(root: Path, sid: str, verdict: str, implies: str) -> Path:
    52	    """Close season `sid`: append its harvest record to akar, return the path."""
    53	    if verdict not in VERDICTS:
    54	        msg = f"verdict must be one of {'|'.join(VERDICTS)}, got '{verdict}'"
    55	        raise ValueError(msg)
    56	    state = engine.read_status(root, sid)
    57	    if state["status"] == "running":
    58	        logger.warning("season %s still running; harvesting a live snapshot", sid)
    59	    record_id = f"{sid}-harvest"
    60	    body = _render_body(state, verdict, implies)
    61	    path = akar.append_record(root, record_id, f"season {sid} harvest", body)
    62	    logger.info("season %s harvested (%s) -> %s", sid, verdict, path)
    63	    return path
     1	"""YAML loading with rumpun's ratified trap fixes (DESIGN.md edge cases).
     2	
     3	- YAML 1.1 booleans: pyyaml parses the key `on:` as True (the `stop.on` trap).
     4	  We reject any mapping key that is not a string.
     5	- Duplicate keys: pyyaml silently keeps the last. We reject duplicates.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import logging
    11	from pathlib import Path
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	import yaml
    15	
    16	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17	
    18	
    19	class YamlError(Exception):
    20	    pass
    21	
    22	
    23	class _UniqueKeyLoader(yaml.SafeLoader):
    24	    pass
    25	
    26	
    27	def _construct_mapping(loader: yaml.Loader, node: yaml.Node, deep: bool = False) -> dict[Any, Any]:
    28	    mapping = {}
    29	    for key_node, value_node in node.value:
    30	        key = loader.construct_object(key_node, deep=deep)
    31	        if key in mapping:
    32	            msg = f"duplicate YAML key {key!r}"
    33	            raise YamlError(msg)
    34	        mapping[key] = loader.construct_object(value_node, deep=deep)
    35	    return mapping
    36	
    37	
    38	_UniqueKeyLoader.add_constructor(
    39	    yaml.resolver.BaseResolver.DEFAULT_MAPPING_TAG, _construct_mapping
    40	)
    41	
    42	
    43	def _reject_non_string_keys(node: Any, path: str) -> None:
    44	    if isinstance(node, dict):
    45	        for key, value in node.items():
    46	            if not isinstance(key, str):
    47	                msg = f"{path}: non-string mapping key {key!r} (YAML 1.1 boolean-key trap)"
    48	                raise YamlError(msg)
    49	            _reject_non_string_keys(value, path)
    50	
    51	
    52	def load(path: Path) -> Any:
    53	    """Load a YAML file with duplicate-key and non-string-key rejection."""
    54	    try:
    55	        data = yaml.load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), Loader=_UniqueKeyLoader)
    56	    except yaml.YAMLError as exc:
    57	        msg = f"{path}: invalid YAML: {exc}"
    58	        raise YamlError(msg) from exc
    59	    if data is None:
    60	        msg = f"{path}: empty YAML document"
    61	        raise YamlError(msg)
    62	    _reject_non_string_keys(data, str(path))
    63	    return data

    94	_LEGEND = """<section class="legend">
    95	<h2>Provenance</h2>
    96	<p class="legend-note">Every value is labeled: where it came from.</p>
    97	<ul>
    98	<li><strong>[H] harness-observed</strong> - recorded by the engine itself:
    99	season identity, status transition, start/end times, route at spawn, exit
   100	codes, wall-clock seconds, artifact existence.</li>
   101	<li><strong>[A] agent-authored</strong> - season YAML text (goal,
   102	primary_change, evidence) and verdicts, quoted verbatim; the harness checks
   103	locators resolve, not that claims are true.</li>
   104	<li><strong>[D] derived</strong> - computed from stated inputs: duration,
   105	agent state policy, strip verdict mapping.</li>
   106	</ul>

   108	
   109	_GLYPHS = {
   110	    "WIN": '<path d="M4 10l4 4 8-8" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5"/>',
   111	    "LOSS": '<path d="M4 4l12 12M16 4L4 16" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5"/>',
   112	    "NEUTRAL": '<path d="M3 10h14" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5"/>',
   113	    "INVALID": '<path d="M10 3v9M10 15v2" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5"/>',
   114	    "no state": '<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7" fill="none" stroke-width="2"/>',
   115	    "running": (
   116	        '<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7" fill="none" stroke-width="2" '
   117	        'stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>'
   118	    ),
   119	}
   120	
   121	
   122	def _esc(value: Any) -> str:
   123	    return html.escape(str(value))
   124	
   125	
   126	def _glyph(name: str, color: str) -> str:
   127	    body = _GLYPHS.get(name, _GLYPHS["no state"])
   128	    return (
   129	        f'<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true" '
   130	        f'stroke="{color}" style="vertical-align:-2px">{body}</svg>'
   131	    )
   132	
   133	
   134	def _verdict_of(root: Path, sid: str) -> str:
   135	    """Season verdict from the last season-level verdicts.jsonl row [H]."""
   136	    verdicts = season_dir(root, sid) / "verdicts.jsonl"
   137	    if not verdicts.is_file():
   138	        return "INVALID"
   139	    last = ""
   140	    with verdicts.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   141	        for line in fh:
   142	            if line.strip():
   143	                try:
   144	                    row = json.loads(line)
   145	                except ValueError:
   146	                    continue
   147	                if row.get("season") == sid:
   148	                    last = str(row.get("verdict", ""))
   149	    return last or "INVALID"
   150	
   151	
   152	def _iso(ts: float | None) -> str:
   153	    if ts is None:
   154	        return "-"
   155	    return datetime.fromtimestamp(ts, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   156	
   157	
   158	def _num(value: float | None, suffix: str = "") -> str:
   159	    if value is None:
   160	        return "-"
   161	    return f"{value:.1f}{suffix}"
   162	
   163	
   164	def _summary_rows(status: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
   165	    started = status.get("started_at")
   166	    ended = status.get("ended_at")
   167	    dur = None if started is None or ended is None else ended - started
   168	    rows = [
   169	        ("Season", "[H]", status.get("id")),
   170	        ("Status", "[H]", status.get("status")),
   171	        ("Started", "[H]", _iso(started)),
   172	        ("Ended", "[H]", _iso(ended)),
   173	        ("Duration", "[D]", _num(dur, " s")),
   174	    ]
   175	    return [
   176	        f'<dt>{_esc(label)} <span class="prov">{_esc(prov)}</span></dt>'
   177	        f"<dd>{_esc(value)}</dd>"
   178	        for label, prov, value in rows
   179	    ]
   180	
   181	
   182	def _agent_rows(agents: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> list[str]:
   183	    rows = []
   184	    for name in sorted(agents):
   185	        a = agents[name]
   186	        cells = [
   187	            a.get("name", name),
   188	            a.get("route", "?"),
   189	            a.get("state", "?"),
   190	            "-" if a.get("exit_code") is None else a["exit_code"],
   191	            _num(a.get("seconds"), " s"),
   192	        ]
   193	        row = "".join(f"<td>{_esc(c)}</td>" for c in cells)
   194	        rows.append(f"<tr>{row}</tr>")
   195	    return rows
   196	
   197	
   198	def _document(status: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   199	    head = "".join(
   200	        f'<th>{_esc(label)} <span class="prov">{_esc(prov)}</span></th>'
   201	        for label, prov in [
   202	            ("Agent", "[H]"), ("Route", "[H]"), ("State", "[D]"),
   203	            ("Exit code", "[H]"), ("Seconds", "[H]"),
   204	        ]
   205	    )
   206	    body = "".join(_agent_rows(status.get("agents") or {}))
   207	    summary = "".join(_summary_rows(status))
   208	    sid = _esc(status.get("id", "?"))
   209	    return (
   210	        "<!DOCTYPE html>\n"
   211	        '<html lang="en">\n<head>\n<meta charset="utf-8">\n'
   212	        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">\n'
   213	        f"<title>rumpun season {sid} report</title>\n"
   214	        f"<style>{_CSS}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n"
   215	        f"<h1>Season {sid}</h1>\n"
   216	        f"<dl>\n{summary}\n</dl>\n"
   217	        f"<table>\n<thead><tr>{head}</tr></thead>\n<tbody>\n{body}\n</tbody>\n</table>\n"
   218	        f"{_POLICY}\n{_LEGEND}\n</body>\n</html>\n"
   219	    )
   220	
   221	
   222	def _season_ids(root: Path) -> list[tuple[int, str]]:
   223	    out: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
   224	    for path in (root / "musim").glob("s*.yaml"):
   225	        match = re.fullmatch(r"s(\d+)", path.stem)
   226	        if match:
   227	            out.append((int(match.group(1)), path.stem))
   228	    return sorted(set(out))
   229	
   230	
   231	def _goal_of(root: Path, sid: str) -> str:
   232	    """The season's goal line, verbatim [A]; empty when absent."""
   233	    season_file = root / "musim" / f"{sid}.yaml"
   234	    if not season_file.is_file():
   235	        return ""
   236	    try:
   237	        doc = yaml.safe_load(season_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   238	    except yaml.YAMLError:
   239	        return ""
   240	    if isinstance(doc, dict) and isinstance(doc.get("goal"), str):
   241	        return doc["goal"]
   242	    return ""
   243	
   244	
   245	def _strip_tally(root: Path, self_sid: str | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
   246	    """Campaign strip cells + tally line. Verdict [D] from verdicts [A]/state [H]."""
   247	    ids = _season_ids(root)
   248	    cells: list[str] = []
   249	    wins = losses = invalids = nostate = 0
   250	    for _, sid_other in ids:
   251	        state_file = state_path(root, sid_other)
   252	        if not state_file.is_file():
   253	            nostate += 1
   254	            color, verdict, dur_txt = C_MUTED, "no state", "-"
   255	        else:
   256	            state = read_status(root, sid_other)
   257	            verdict = _verdict_of(root, sid_other)
   258	            if verdict == "WIN":
   259	                wins += 1
   260	            elif verdict == "LOSS":
   261	                losses += 1
   262	            else:
   263	                invalids += 1
   264	            color = VERDICT_COLOR.get(verdict, C_NEUTRAL)
   265	            dur = (state.get("ended_at") or 0) - state.get("started_at", 0)
   266	            dur_txt = f"{dur:.0f}s" if dur > 0 else "-"
   267	        if sid_other == self_sid:
   268	            link = "report.html"
   269	        elif (season_dir(root, sid_other) / "report.html").is_file():
   270	            link = f"../{sid_other}/report.html"
   271	        else:
   272	            link = None
   273	        inner = (
   274	            '<div class="cell">'
   275	            f'{_glyph(verdict, color)} <span class="sid">{_esc(sid_other)}</span><br>'
   276	            f'<span class="ver" style="color:{color}">{_esc(verdict)}</span> '
   277	            f'<span class="ver" style="color:{C_MUTED}">{_esc(dur_txt)}</span>'
   278	            "</div>"
   279	        )
   280	        cells.append(f'<a href="{_esc(link)}">{inner}</a>' if link else inner)
   281	    strip = f'<div class="strip">{"".join(cells)}</div>'
   282	    tally = (
   283	        f'<p class="plain">Campaign tally [D]: {wins} WIN &middot; {losses} LOSS '
   284	        f"&middot; {invalids} NEUTRAL/INVALID &middot; {nostate} no state"
   285	        f" &middot; {len(ids)} seasons</p>"
   286	    )
   287	    return strip, tally
   288	
   289	
   290	def _campaign_section(root: Path, sid: str) -> str:
   291	    """Campaign strip + this season's declared change. [H] file facts, [A] yaml text."""
   292	    strip, tally = _strip_tally(root, sid)
   293	    change_rows: list[str] = []
   294	    season_file = root / "musim" / f"{sid}.yaml"
   295	    if season_file.is_file():
   296	        try:
   297	            doc = yaml.safe_load(season_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   298	        except yaml.YAMLError:
   299	            doc = None
   300	        if isinstance(doc, dict):
   301	            method = doc.get("methodology") or {}
   302	            change = method.get("primary_change") or {}
   303	            evidence = method.get("evidence") or []
   304	            for field in ("type", "node", "baseline", "expected_band", "eval_window"):
   305	                if change.get(field):
   306	                    change_rows.append(
   307	                        f"<dt>{_esc(field)} <span class='prov'>[A]</span></dt>"
   308	                        f"<dd>{_esc(change[field])}</dd>"
   309	                    )
   310	            for cite in evidence:
   311	                if isinstance(cite, str):
   312	                    change_rows.append(
   313	                        f"<dt>evidence <span class='prov'>[A]</span></dt>"
   314	                        f"<dd>{_esc(cite)}</dd>"
   315	                    )
   316	    change_html = ""
   317	    if change_rows:
   318	        change_html = (
   319	            '<section class="card change"><h2>Declared change this season</h2>'
   320	            f"<dl>{''.join(change_rows)}</dl></section>"
   321	        )
   322	    durations: list[tuple[str, float, str]] = []
   323	    for _, sid_other in _season_ids(root):
   324	        state_file = state_path(root, sid_other)
   325	        if not state_file.is_file():
   326	            continue
   327	        state = read_status(root, sid_other)
   328	        dur = (state.get("ended_at") or 0) - state.get("started_at", 0)
   329	        if dur > 0:
   330	            durations.append((sid_other, dur, _verdict_of(root, sid_other)))
   331	    bars = ""
   332	    if len(durations) >= 4:
   333	        max_dur = max(d for _, d, _ in durations) or 1
   334	        bar_rows = []
   335	        for sid_other, dur, verdict in durations:
   336	            width = max(2, int(dur / max_dur * 400))
   337	            cls = {"WIN": "barwin", "LOSS": "barloss"}.get(verdict, "bar")
   338	            bar_rows.append(
   339	                f"<text x='0' y='12' font-size='10' fill='{C_MUTED}'>"
   340	                f"{_esc(sid_other)}</text>"
   341	                f"<rect class='{cls}' x='46' y='4' width='{width}' height='10'/>"
   342	                f"<text x='{52 + width}' y='12' font-size='10' fill='{C_MUTED}'>"
   343	                f"{dur:.0f}s {_esc(verdict)}</text>"
   344	            )
   345	        bars = (
   346	            '<section class="card"><h2>Duration per season [H]</h2><svg '
   347	            f"width='560' height='{28 * len(durations)}' role='img' "
   348	            "aria-label='bar chart of season durations in seconds'>"
   349	            + "".join(
   350	                f"<g transform='translate(0,{28 * i})'>{row}</g>"
   351	                for i, row in enumerate(bar_rows)
   352	            )
   353	            + "</svg></section>"
   354	        )
   355	    return (
   356	        '<section class="card"><h2>Campaign progress</h2>'
   357	        f"{strip}{tally}</section>{change_html}{bars}"
   358	    )
   359	
   360	
   361	_ROUTINE_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^s\d+-harvest$")
   362	_GATE_PREFIXES = ("approve-", "reject-", "rollback-")
   363	
   364	
   365	def _write_atomic(path: Path, doc: str) -> None:

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse Completed
hook: PreToolUse
hook: PreToolUse Completed
exec
/usr/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/rumpun/report.py | sed -n '366,667p'" in /mnt/data/work/rumpun
 succeeded in 0ms:
   366	    """Atomic render write: tmp file then replace (report convention)."""
   367	    tmp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
   368	    tmp.write_text(doc, encoding="utf-8")
   369	    tmp.replace(path)

   371	
   372	def _discovery_records(root: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   373	    """Non-routine akar records: the campaign's discoveries [A].
   374	
   375	    A discovery is any top-level akar/*.md whose id is not routine
   376	    bookkeeping: <sid>-harvest rows and approve/reject/rollback gate
   377	    paperwork are excluded; audit reflections, defect records, and
   378	    workarounds are the interesting feed. Sorted by filename (date
   379	    prefix), oldest first.
   380	    """
   381	    found: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   382	    akar_dir = root / "akar"
   383	    if not akar_dir.is_dir():
   384	        return found
   385	    for path in sorted(akar_dir.glob("*.md")):
   386	        rid = title = date = ""
   387	        try:
   388	            head = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[:10]
   389	        except OSError as exc:
   390	            logger.warning("skip unreadable akar record %s: %s", path, exc)
   391	            continue
   392	        for line in head:
   393	            if line.startswith("id: ") and not rid:
   394	                rid = line[4:].strip()
   395	            elif line.startswith("title: ") and not title:
   396	                title = line[7:].strip()
   397	            elif line.startswith("date: ") and not date:
   398	                date = line[6:].strip()
   399	        if not rid:
   400	            rid = path.stem
   401	        if _ROUTINE_ID_RE.match(rid) or rid.startswith(_GATE_PREFIXES):
   402	            continue
   403	        found.append(
   404	            {"id": rid, "title": title or rid, "date": date, "path": path.name}
   405	        )
   406	    return found
   407	
   408	
   409	def render_discoveries(root: Path, out: Path | None = None) -> Path:
   410	    """Discoveries list + one verbatim page per record under rimba/discoveries/.
   411	
   412	    Deterministic; never mutates the ledger (akar is read-only here).
   413	    Returns the list page path.
   414	    """
   415	    records = _discovery_records(root)
   416	    out_dir = root / "rimba" / "discoveries"
   417	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   418	    items = []
   419	    for rec in reversed(records):
   420	        items.append(
   421	            f'<li><a href="{_esc(rec["id"])}.html">{_esc(rec["title"])}</a> '
   422	            f'<span class="prov">{_esc(rec["id"])} &middot; '
   423	            f'{_esc(rec["date"])}</span></li>'
   424	        )
   425	    listing = (
   426	        "<!DOCTYPE html>\n"
   427	        '<html lang="en">\n<head>\n<meta charset="utf-8">\n'
   428	        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">\n'
   429	        "<title>rumpun discoveries</title>\n"
   430	        f"<style>{_CSS}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n"
   431	        "<h1>Discoveries</h1>\n"
   432	        '<p class="plain">What the campaign learned about itself and its '
   433	        'tools <span class="prov">[A]</span> &middot; '
   434	        '<a href="../index.html">all progress</a></p>\n'
   435	        f"<ul>{''.join(items)}</ul>\n{_POLICY}\n</body>\n</html>\n"
   436	    )
   437	    list_path = out if out is not None else out_dir / "index.html"
   438	    _write_atomic(list_path, listing)
   439	    for rec in records:
   440	        body = (root / "akar" / rec["path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   441	        page = (
   442	            "<!DOCTYPE html>\n"
   443	            '<html lang="en">\n<head>\n<meta charset="utf-8">\n'
   444	            '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">\n'
   445	            f"<title>rumpun discovery {rec['id']}</title>\n"
   446	            f"<style>{_CSS}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n"
   447	            f"<h1>{_esc(rec['title'])}</h1>\n"
   448	            '<p class="plain">'
   449	            f'<span class="prov">{_esc(rec["id"])} &middot; {_esc(rec["date"])}'
   450	            ' [A]</span> &middot; '
   451	            '<a href="index.html">all discoveries</a></p>\n'
   452	            f"<pre>{_esc(body)}</pre>\n{_POLICY}\n</body>\n</html>\n"
   453	        )
   454	        safe = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9-]", "-", rec["id"].lower())
   455	        _write_atomic(out_dir / f"{safe}.html", page)
   456	    return list_path
   457	
   458	
   459	def render_index(root: Path, out: Path | None = None) -> Path:
   460	    """Landing page: plain-language progress first, details one click away.
   461	
   462	    Deterministic like every view: no clock reads, sorted orders. The human
   463	    sentences quote season goal lines verbatim [A]; counts and states are
   464	    [H]/[D]. Never mutates anything.
   465	    """
   466	    ids = _season_ids(root)
   467	    closed: list[tuple[str, str, float, str]] = []
   468	    running: list[str] = []
   469	    for _, sid_other in ids:
   470	        state_file = state_path(root, sid_other)
   471	        if not state_file.is_file():
   472	            continue
   473	        state = read_status(root, sid_other)
   474	        dur = (state.get("ended_at") or 0) - state.get("started_at", 0)
   475	        if state.get("status") == "running":
   476	            running.append(sid_other)
   477	        elif dur > 0:
   478	            closed.append(
   479	                (sid_other, _verdict_of(root, sid_other), dur, _goal_of(root, sid_other))
   480	            )
   481	    wins = sum(1 for _, v, _, _ in closed if v == "WIN")
   482	    losses = sum(1 for _, v, _, _ in closed if v == "LOSS")
   483	    goal = _goal_of(root, ids[-1][1]) if ids else ""
   484	    if closed:
   485	        last_sid, last_v, last_dur, last_goal = closed[-1]
   486	        last_line = (
   487	            f'<p class="plain">Last finished: <strong>{_esc(last_sid)} {_esc(last_v)}'
   488	            f"</strong> in {last_dur:.0f}s &mdash; built: {_esc(last_goal or '(no goal line)')}"
   489	            " <span class='prov'>[A]</span></p>"
   490	        )
   491	    else:
   492	        last_line = '<p class="plain idle">Nothing finished yet.</p>'
   493	    if running:
   494	        rid = running[-1]
   495	        run_state = read_status(root, rid)
   496	        if (season_dir(root, rid) / "report.html").is_file():
   497	            who = (
   498	                f'<a href="{_esc(rid)}/report.html">'
   499	                f"<strong>{_esc(rid)}</strong></a>"
   500	            )
   501	        else:
   502	            who = f"<strong>{_esc(rid)}</strong>"
   503	        building = (
   504	            f'<p class="plain">Building right now: {who}'
   505	            f" &mdash; {_esc(_goal_of(root, rid) or '(no goal line)')}"
   506	            f" <span class='prov'>[A]</span> &middot; started "
   507	            f"{_esc(_iso(run_state.get('started_at')))} [H]</p>"
   508	        )
   509	    else:
   510	        building = (
   511	            '<p class="plain idle">Idle &mdash; nothing is building right now.</p>'
   512	        )
   513	    recent = ""
   514	    if closed:
   515	        items = []
   516	        for sid_other, verdict, dur, goal_line in reversed(closed[-3:]):
   517	            color = VERDICT_COLOR.get(verdict, C_NEUTRAL)
   518	            items.append(
   519	                f"<li>{_glyph(verdict, color)} <strong>{_esc(sid_other)} "
   520	                f"{_esc(verdict)}</strong> ({dur:.0f}s) &mdash; "
   521	                f"{_esc(goal_line or '(no goal line)')}</li>"
   522	            )
   523	        recent = (
   524	            '<section class="card"><h2>Latest results</h2><ul>' 
   525	            + "".join(items)
   526	            + "</ul></section>"
   527	        )
   528	    strip, tally = _strip_tally(root, None)
   529	    render_discoveries(root)
   530	    discoveries = _discovery_records(root)
   531	    disc_card = ""
   532	    if discoveries:
   533	        links = "".join(
   534	            f'<li><a href="discoveries/{_esc(rec["id"])}.html">'
   535	            f'{_esc(rec["title"])}</a> '
   536	            f'<span class="prov">{_esc(rec["date"])}</span></li>'
   537	            for rec in reversed(discoveries[-5:])
   538	        )
   539	        disc_card = (
   540	            '<section class="card"><h2>Discoveries [A]</h2><ul>'
   541	            f"{links}</ul>"
   542	            '<p class="plain"><a href="discoveries/index.html">'
   543	            f"all {len(discoveries)} discoveries</a></p></section>"
   544	        )
   545	    head = "<h1>rumpun progress</h1>"
   546	    if goal:
   547	        head += f'<p class="plain">Campaign: {_esc(goal)} <span class="prov">[A]</span></p>'
   548	    summary = (
   549	        f'<p class="plain"><strong>{len(closed)}</strong> seasons built &middot; '
   550	        f'<strong>{wins}</strong> WIN &middot; <strong>{losses}</strong> LOSS'
   551	        " <span class='prov'>[D]</span></p>"
   552	    )
   553	    doc = (
   554	        "<!DOCTYPE html>\n"
   555	        '<html lang="en">\n<head>\n<meta charset="utf-8">\n'
   556	        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">\n'
   557	        "<title>rumpun progress</title>\n"
   558	        f"<style>{_CSS}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n"
   559	        f"{head}{summary}{building}{last_line}{recent}{disc_card}"
   560	        '<section class="card"><h2>Every season</h2>'
   561	        f"{strip}{tally}</section>"
   562	        f"{_POLICY}\n{_LEGEND}\n"
   563	        f'<p class="footer">project {_esc(root.parent.name)} [H] &middot; '
   564	        "deterministic static view\n"
   565	        "</body>\n</html>\n"
   566	    )
   567	    target = out if out is not None else root / "rimba" / "index.html"
   568	    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   569	    tmp = target.with_suffix(".tmp")
   570	    tmp.write_text(doc, encoding="utf-8")
   571	    tmp.replace(target)
   572	    logger.info("campaign index -> %s", target)
   573	    return target
   574	
   575	
   576	def _footer(project: str, sid: str, state_sha: str) -> str:
   577	    """Self-identification line [H]: which project, season, and state bytes."""
   578	    return (
   579	        f'<p class="footer">project {_esc(project)} · season {_esc(sid)} · '
   580	        f"state sha256:{state_sha[:12]} [H]</p>"
   581	    )
   582	
   583	
   584	def _workspace_files(ws: Path) -> list[str]:
   585	    """Workspace files worth linking: agent.log plus every deliverable.
   586	
   587	    A file is a deliverable when no component of its workspace-relative
   588	    path is in audit's BOOKKEEPING_FILES (the audit's set — one source of
   589	    truth); agent.log is bookkeeping but linked anyway: it is the agent's
   590	    full stream.
   591	    """
   592	    found = ["agent.log"] if (ws / "agent.log").is_file() else []
   593	    if ws.is_dir():
   594	        for path in ws.rglob("*"):
   595	            if not path.is_file():
   596	                continue
   597	            rel = path.relative_to(ws)
   598	            if any(part in BOOKKEEPING_FILES for part in rel.parts):
   599	                continue
   600	            found.append(rel.as_posix())
   601	    return found
   602	
   603	
   604	def _workspaces_section(root: Path, sid: str, status: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   605	    """Per-agent workspace links [D]: the log stream plus deliverables."""
   606	    agents = status.get("agents") or {}
   607	    if not agents:
   608	        return ""
   609	    rows = []
   610	    for name in sorted(agents):
   611	        files = _workspace_files(season_dir(root, sid) / name)
   612	        links = (
   613	            " ".join(
   614	                f'<a href="{_esc(name)}/{_esc(f)}">{_esc(f)}</a>' for f in files
   615	            )
   616	            if files
   617	            else "-"
   618	        )
   619	        rows.append(f"<tr><td>{_esc(name)}</td><td>{links}</td></tr>")
   620	    return (
   621	        '<section class="card"><h2>Workspaces [D]</h2>'
   622	        "<table><thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Files</th></tr></thead>"
   623	        f"<tbody>{''.join(rows)}</tbody></table></section>"
   624	    )
   625	
   626	
   627	def render_report(root: Path, sid: str, out: Path | None = None) -> Path:
   628	    """Render the dashboard page for season `sid`; returns its path.
   629	
   630	    Default target: root/rimba/<sid>/report.html (or `out`). Never mutates
   631	    season state; read_status failures propagate as EngineError. The page
   632	    self-identifies with the sha256 of the state.json bytes: same bytes,
   633	    same page.
   634	    """
   635	    status = read_status(root, sid)
   636	    state_sha = hashlib.sha256(state_path(root, sid).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
   637	    footer = _footer(root.parent.name, sid, state_sha)
   638	    doc = _document(status)
   639	    campaign = _campaign_section(root, sid)
   640	    workspaces = _workspaces_section(root, sid, status)
   641	    doc = doc.replace(
   642	        _POLICY,
   643	        f"{campaign}{workspaces}{_POLICY}",
   644	    )
   645	    verdict = _verdict_of(root, sid)
   646	    dur = (status.get("ended_at") or 0) - status.get("started_at", 0)
   647	    if dur > 0:
   648	        head_line = (
   649	            f'<p class="one">{_glyph(verdict, VERDICT_COLOR.get(verdict, C_NEUTRAL))} '
   650	            f"<strong>{_esc(verdict) or _esc(status.get('status'))}</strong> &mdash; "
   651	            f"built: {_esc(_goal_of(root, sid) or '(no goal line)')}"
   652	            f" &mdash; {dur:.0f}s <span class='prov'>[A][D]</span> &middot; "
   653	            f"<a href='../index.html'>all progress</a></p>"
   654	        )
   655	        doc = doc.replace(
   656	            f"<h1>Season {_esc(status.get('id', '?'))}</h1>\n",
   657	            f"<h1>Season {_esc(status.get('id', '?'))}</h1>\n{head_line}\n",
   658	            1,
   659	        )
   660	    doc = doc.replace("</body>", f"{footer}\n</body>")
   661	    target = out if out is not None else season_dir(root, sid) / "report.html"
   662	    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   663	    tmp = target.with_suffix(".tmp")
   664	    tmp.write_text(doc, encoding="utf-8")
   665	    tmp.replace(target)  # atomic write
   666	    logger.info("season %s report -> %s", sid, target)
   667	    return target

    61	def _find_config(start: Path) -> Path | None:
    62	    for candidate in (start, *start.parents):
    63	        if (candidate / ".rumpun" / "rumpun.yaml").is_file():
    64	            return candidate / ".rumpun" / "rumpun.yaml"
    65	    return None

    68	def _project_root(start: Path) -> Path:
    69	    cfg = _find_config(start)
    70	    if cfg is None:
    71	        msg = "no .rumpun/rumpun.yaml found above cwd; run 'rumpun init' first"
    72	        raise lint_mod.LintError(msg)
    73	    return cfg.parent

    83	def cmd_lint(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    84	    findings = lint_mod.lint(Path(args.file).resolve())
    85	    errors = [f for f in findings if f.severity == "error"]
    86	    for f in findings:
    87	        line = f"{f.path}: {f.message}" if f.path else f.message
    88	        (logger.error if f.severity == "error" else logger.warning)(line)
    89	    if errors:
    90	        warnings = len(findings) - len(errors)
    91	        logger.error("lint FAILED: %d error(s), %d warning(s)", len(errors), warnings)
    92	        return 1
    93	    logger.info("lint OK (%d warning(s))", len(findings) - len(errors))
    94	    return 0

   102	def cmd_models(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   103	    """Local agent-route detector (build order step 2). Read-only by
   104	    default; --write fills rumpun.yaml routes; --probe spends quota."""
   105	    for row in routes_mod.detect_clis():
   106	        mark = "yes" if row["present"] == "yes" else "no "
   107	        ver = f" ({row['version']})" if row["version"] else ""
   108	        print(f"  {row['name']:<10} {mark}{ver}")
   109	    print("\nclaude-binary routes (this machine):")
   110	    for route in routes_mod.claude_routes():
   111	        print(f"  [{route['family']:<8}] {route['desc']}")
   112	        print(f"      spawn: {route['command']}")
   113	    print("\ncodex routes (models_cache when present, else default):")
   114	    for route in routes_mod.codex_model_routes():
   115	        print(f"  [{route['family']:<12}] {route['desc']}")
   116	        print(f"      spawn: {route['command']}")
   117	    if getattr(args, "write", False):
   118	        cfg = _find_config(Path.cwd())
   119	        if cfg is None:
   120	            logger.error("--write needs a rumpun project (no rumpun.yaml above cwd)")
   121	            return 1
   122	        count = routes_mod.write_routes(cfg)
   123	        yamlio.load(cfg)  # re-parse: a broken patch must fail loudly here
   124	        logger.info("wrote %d routes into %s", count, cfg)
   125	    if getattr(args, "probe", False):
   126	        print("\n--probe: one tiny completion per claude route (spends quota)...")
   127	        for route in routes_mod.claude_routes():
   128	            cmd = route["command"].replace("<model>", "fable")
   129	            if "{prompt}" in cmd:
   130	                cmd = cmd.replace("{prompt}", "'Reply with exactly: READY'")
   131	            t0 = time.monotonic()
   132	            try:
   133	                out = subprocess.run(
   134	                    cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
   135	                    timeout=90, check=False,
   136	                )
   137	                dt = time.monotonic() - t0
   138	                lines = (out.stdout or "").strip().splitlines()
   139	                reply = lines[-1][:60] if lines else "(empty)"
   140	                ok = "yes" if out.returncode == 0 and reply != "(empty)" else "NO"
   141	                print(f"  {ok} [{route['family']}] {dt:.1f}s — {reply}")
   142	            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   143	                print(f"  NO [{route['family']}] timeout (90s)")
   144	    return 0

   147	def cmd_direct(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   148	    """P8 directives: the human seat's channel. Appends only touch the
   149	    artifact; nothing is injected into a running process, and the jsonl
   150	    records pending versus consumed."""
   151	    root = _project_root(Path.cwd())
   152	    lane = {
   153	        "file": str(root / "akar" / "directives.jsonl"),
   154	        "lock": str(root / "akar" / "directives.lock"),
   155	    }
   156	    if args.list:
   157	        path = Path(lane["file"])
   158	        events = collab.read_events(lane) if path.is_file() else []
   159	        if not events:
   160	            print("no directives")
   161	            return 0
   162	        pending = [e for e in events if e.get("status") == "pending"]
   163	        consumed = [e for e in events if e.get("status") != "pending"]
   164	        for event in pending + consumed:
   165	            print(f"{event['seq']}  {event.get('status')}  {event.get('text')}")
   166	        return 0
   167	    if not args.text:
   168	        logger.error("direct requires TEXT (append) or --list")
   169	        return 2
   170	    event = collab.append_event(
   171	        lane, "operator", {"text": args.text, "status": "pending"}
   172	    )
   173	    print(f"{event['seq']}  {event['status']}  {event['text']}")
   174	    return 0

   176	
   177	def _render_state(state: dict) -> str:
   178	    """Human table for a season snapshot (P36 surface 1, snapshot form)."""
   179	    started = time.strftime("%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(state["started_at"]))
   180	    end = state.get("ended_at") or time.time()
   181	    dur = end - state["started_at"]
   182	    lines = [f"season {state['id']}: {state['status']} ({dur:.0f}s, started {started})"]
   183	    agents = state.get("agents") or {}
   184	    for name in sorted(agents):
   185	        a = agents[name]
   186	        code = "" if a.get("exit_code") is None else f" code {a['exit_code']}"
   187	        lines.append(
   188	            f"  {a['name']:<8} {a['route']:<14} {a['state']:<11}"
   189	            f"{code} {a['seconds']}s"
   190	        )
   191	    return "\n".join(lines)

   194	def _lint_preflight(yaml_path: Path) -> bool:
   195	    """P27 boundary: errors block start. Returns True when clean."""
   196	    findings = lint_mod.lint(yaml_path)
   197	    errors = [f for f in findings if f.severity == "error"]
   198	    for f in findings:
   199	        line = f"{f.path}: {f.message}" if f.path else f.message
   200	        (logger.error if f.severity == "error" else logger.warning)(line)
   201	    return not errors
   202

   204	def cmd_season_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   205	    yaml_path = Path(args.file).resolve()
   206	    if not _lint_preflight(yaml_path):
   207	        logger.error("lint errors block season start (P27 preflight)")
   208	        return 1
   209	    root = _project_root(yaml_path.parent)
   210	    state = engine.start_season(yaml_path, root)
   211	    if getattr(args, "json", False):
   212	        print(json.dumps(state, indent=2))
   213	    else:
   214	        print(_render_state(state))
   215	    return 0

   228	def _deliverables(ws: Path) -> list[str]:
   229	    """Relative names of files in an agent workspace, minus engine bookkeeping."""
   230	    if not ws.is_dir():
   231	        return []
   232	    out: list[str] = []
   233	    for path in sorted(ws.rglob("*")):
   234	        rel = path.relative_to(ws)
   235	        if path.is_file() and not (BOOKKEEPING & set(rel.parts)):
   236	            out.append(rel.as_posix())
   237	    return out
   238	
   239	
   240	def _lanes(sdir: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
   241	    """Non-blank event count per lane-*.jsonl in the season _season dir."""
   242	    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   243	    for path in sorted(sdir.glob("lane-*.jsonl")):
   244	        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   245	            counts[path.name] = sum(1 for line in fh if line.strip())
   246	    return counts
   247

   249	def cmd_season_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   250	    root = _project_root(Path.cwd())
   251	    state = engine.read_status(root, args.id)  # unknown id -> EngineError
   252	    sdir = engine.season_dir(root, args.id)
   253	    agents = state.get("agents") or {}
   254	    deliverables = {name: _deliverables(sdir / name) for name in sorted(agents)}
   255	    lanes = _lanes(sdir / "_season")
   256	    if getattr(args, "json", False):
   257	        payload = {
   258	            "id": state["id"],
   259	            "status": state["status"],
   260	            "started_at": state["started_at"],
   261	            "ended_at": state.get("ended_at"),
   262	            "agents": agents,
   263	            "deliverables": deliverables,
   264	            "lanes": lanes,
   265	        }
   266	        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
   267	        return 0
   268	    print(_render_state(state).splitlines()[0])
   269	    for name, files in deliverables.items():
   270	        listed = ", ".join(files) if files else "(no deliverables)"
   271	        print(f"  {name}: {listed}")
   272	    for lane, count in lanes.items():
   273	        print(f"  {lane}  {count} events")
   274	    return 0

   277	def _season_sort_key(path: Path) -> tuple[int, str]:
   278	    m = re.fullmatch(r"s(\d+)", path.stem)
   279	    return (int(m.group(1)) if m else 0, path.stem)

   282	def cmd_season_list(_args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:

   306	def _serve_rimba(rimba: Path, sid: str) -> int:
   307	    """Serve the rimba tree on 127.0.0.1:8611 until Ctrl-C; exit 0."""
   308	    handler = functools.partial(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(rimba))
   309	    try:
   310	        with ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8611), handler) as httpd:
   311	            logger.info("serving %s at http://127.0.0.1:8611 (^C to stop)", rimba)
   312	            print(f"http://localhost:8611/{sid}/report.html")
   313	            httpd.serve_forever()
   314	    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   315	        logger.info("serve stopped")
   316	    return 0

   400	def _not_implemented(name: str, what: str):
   401	    def _run(_args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   402	        logger.error("'%s' is not implemented yet: %s", name, what)
   403	        return 2
   404	
   405	    return _run
   406	
   407	
   408	def _add_stub(sub: argparse.ArgumentParser, name: str, what: str) -> None:
   409	    p = sub.add_parser(name, help=what)
   410	    p.set_defaults(func=_not_implemented(name, what))
   411	
   412	
   413	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
   414	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rumpun", description=__doc__)
   415	    parser.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=__version__)
   416	    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
   417	
   418	    p_init = sub.add_parser("init", help="scaffold a rumpun project in TARGET")
   419	    p_init.add_argument("target", nargs="?", default=".")
   420	    p_init.set_defaults(func=cmd_init)
   421	
   422	    for verb, func, help_text in [
   423	        ("lint", cmd_lint, "validate a season YAML (structure, DAG, contracts, citations)"),
   424	        ("graph", cmd_graph, "render a season pipeline as mermaid"),
   425	        ("models", cmd_models, "detect spawnable model routes on this machine"),
   426	    ]:
   427	        p = sub.add_parser(verb, help=help_text)
   428	        if verb == "models":
   429	            p.add_argument("--write", action="store_true",
   430	                           help="fill rumpun.yaml routes: {} with the detected table")
   431	            p.add_argument("--probe", action="store_true",
   432	                           help="send one tiny completion per claude route (spends quota)")
   433	        else:
   434	            p.add_argument("file", help="season YAML path")
   435	        p.set_defaults(func=func)
   436	
   437	    p_direct = sub.add_parser(
   438	        "direct", help="append or list operator directives (P8)",
   439	    )
   440	    p_direct.add_argument(
   441	        "text", nargs="?", help="directive text; recorded with status pending",
   442	    )
   443	    p_direct.add_argument(
   444	        "--list", action="store_true", help="print all directives, pending first",
   445	    )
   446	    p_direct.set_defaults(func=cmd_direct)
   447	
   448	    p_board = sub.add_parser("board", help="live season board (P36: snapshot form)")
   449	    p_board.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s1")
   450	    p_board.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_status)
   451	
   452	    p_harvest = sub.add_parser("harvest", help="tuai: close a season into akar (step 4)")
   453	    p_harvest.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s2")
   454	    p_harvest.add_argument("--verdict", required=True,
   455	                           choices=["WIN", "LOSS", "NEUTRAL", "INVALID"])
   456	    p_harvest.add_argument("--implies", required=True)
   457	    p_harvest.set_defaults(func=cmd_harvest)
   458	
   459	    p_audit = sub.add_parser(
   460	        "audit",
   461	        help="reflect over the season ledger; append evidence-cited candidates (DESIGN 15)",
   462	    )
   463	    p_audit.add_argument(
   464	        "--last", type=int, default=10, metavar="N",
   465	        help="audit the last N musim seasons (default: 10)",
   466	    )
   467	    p_audit.set_defaults(func=cmd_audit)
   468	
   469	    for verb, what in NOT_IMPLEMENTED.items():
   470	        _add_stub(sub, verb, what)
   471	
   472	    p_season = sub.add_parser("season", help="season lifecycle commands")
   473	    season_sub = p_season.add_subparsers(dest="season_command", required=True)
   474	    p_start = season_sub.add_parser("start", help="spawn the season (build order step 3)")
   475	    p_start.add_argument("file", help="season YAML path")
   476	    p_start.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="machine-readable output")
   477	    p_start.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_start)
   478	    p_status = season_sub.add_parser("status", help="season snapshot with --json (P36 priority 0)")
   479	    p_status.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s1")
   480	    p_status.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="machine-readable output")
   481	    p_status.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_status)
   482	    p_list = season_sub.add_parser("list", help="one line per season: id, status, duration, agents")
   483	    p_list.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_list)
   484	    p_stop = season_sub.add_parser("stop", help="stop the season (build order step 3)")
   485	    p_stop.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s1")
   486	    p_stop.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_stop)
   487	    p_report = season_sub.add_parser("report", help="static HTML season report (P36 surface 2)")
   488	    p_report.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s2")
   489	    p_report.add_argument(
   490	        "--serve", action="store_true",
   491	        help="after rendering, serve rimba/ over HTTP at http://localhost:8611",
   492	    )
   493	    p_report.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_report)
   494	    p_show = season_sub.add_parser(
   495	        "show", help="season detail: status line, deliverables, lanes",
   496	    )
   497	    p_show.add_argument("id", help="season id, e.g. s1")
   498	    p_show.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="machine-readable output")
   499	    p_show.set_defaults(func=cmd_season_show)
   500	    if SEASON_STUBS:
   501	        for verb, what in SEASON_STUBS.items():
   502	            _add_stub(season_sub, verb, what)
   503	
   504	    p_evolve = sub.add_parser("evolve", help="evolution commands (evolusi)")
   505	    evolve_sub = p_evolve.add_subparsers(dest="evolve_command", required=True)
   506	    p_plan = evolve_sub.add_parser("plan", help="draft the next season YAML (build order step 5)")
   507	    p_plan.add_argument("file", help="parent season YAML path")
   508	    p_plan.set_defaults(func=cmd_evolve_plan)
   509	    p_apply = evolve_sub.add_parser("apply", help="lint-gate a drafted season")
   510	    p_apply.add_argument("file", help="drafted season YAML path")
   511	    p_apply.set_defaults(func=cmd_evolve_apply)
   512	    p_approve = evolve_sub.add_parser(
   513	        "approve", help="approve a drafted evolution; appends the akar approval record",
   514	    )
   515	    p_approve.add_argument("file", help="drafted season YAML path")
   516	    p_approve.set_defaults(func=cmd_evolve_approve)
   517	    p_reject = evolve_sub.add_parser(
   518	        "reject", help="reject a drafted evolution; triggers the on_reject policy (P33)",
   519	    )
   520	    p_reject.add_argument("file", help="drafted season YAML path")
   521	    p_reject.set_defaults(func=cmd_evolve_reject)
   522	    p_rollback = evolve_sub.add_parser(
   523	        "rollback",
   524	        help="contain an applied season; on_reject policy records in akar (P33)",
   525	    )
   526	    p_rollback.add_argument("id", help="applied season id, e.g. s3")
   527	    p_rollback.set_defaults(func=cmd_evolve_rollback)
   528	
   529	    return parser
   530	
   531	
   532	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   533	    _setup_logging()
   534	    parser = build_parser()
   535	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   536	    try:
   537	        return args.func(args)
   538	    except (lint_mod.LintError, scaffold.ScaffoldError, yamlio.YamlError,
   539	            routes_mod.RoutesError, engine.EngineError, collab.LaneError,
   540	            evolve_mod.EvolveError, akar.AkarError, audit_mod.AuditError) as exc:
   541	        logger.error("%s", exc)
   542	        return 1
   543	
   544	
   545	if __name__ == "__main__":
   546	    sys.exit(main())
