     1	"""rumpun lint — the P27 preflight boundary.
     2	
     3	Checks a season YAML before anything spawns: structure, DAG acyclicity, artifact
     4	contracts, evidence citations, primary_change declaration, benih sanity, autonomy
     5	invariants. Errors block start; warnings do not.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import logging
    11	import re
    12	from dataclasses import dataclass
    13	from pathlib import Path
    14	from typing import Any
    15	
    16	from rumpun import yamlio
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	PRIMITIVES = {
    21	    "analyze", "rank_gaps", "hypothesize", "plan", "falsify",
    22	    "execute", "evaluate", "share", "harvest", "audit", "research",
    23	}
    24	CHANGE_TYPES = {"add", "remove", "rewire", "retune"}
    25	BUNDLE_CLASSES = {"pipeline_switch", "emergency", "rollback_restore", "free_bundle"}
    26	STAGES = {"manual", "panel", "free"}
    27	KNOWLEDGE = {"none", "partial", "full"}
    28	REQUIRED_INVARIANTS = {"goal_immutable", "budget_cap", "falsify_required"}
    29	ID_RE = re.compile(r"^s\d+$")
    30	
    31	
    32	@dataclass
    33	class Finding:
    34	    severity: str  # "error" | "warning"
    35	    message: str
    36	    path: str = ""
    37	
    38	
    39	class LintError(Exception):
    40	    pass
    41	
    42	
    43	def _find_root(start: Path) -> Path:
    44	    for candidate in (start, *start.parents):
    45	        if (candidate / ".rumpun" / "rumpun.yaml").is_file():
    46	            return candidate / ".rumpun"
    47	    msg = f"no .rumpun/rumpun.yaml found above {start}; run 'rumpun init' first"
    48	    raise LintError(msg)
    49	
    50	
    51	def _artifact_producers(pipeline: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, str]:
    52	    producers: dict[str, str] = {}
    53	    for node in pipeline:
    54	        phase = node.get("phase", "?")
    55	        writes = node.get("writes")
    56	        for artifact in [writes] if isinstance(writes, str) else (writes or []):
    57	            if artifact in producers:
    58	                msg = f"two nodes write {artifact}: {producers[artifact]} and {phase}"
    59	                raise LintError(msg)
    60	            producers[artifact] = phase
    61	    return producers
    62	
    63	
    64	def _dag_edges(pipeline: list[dict[str, Any]], producers: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    65	    edges: dict[str, set[str]] = {node["phase"]: set() for node in pipeline}
    66	    for node in pipeline:
    67	        reads = node.get("reads")
    68	        for artifact in [reads] if isinstance(reads, str) else (reads or []):
    69	            producer = producers.get(artifact)
    70	            if producer is None:
    71	                msg = f"node '{node['phase']}' reads {artifact}: nothing writes it"
    72	                raise LintError(msg)
    73	            if producer != node["phase"]:
    74	                edges[producer].add(node["phase"])
    75	    return edges
    76	
    77	
    78	def _topo_order(edges: dict[str, set[str]]) -> list[str]:
    79	    indegree = {n: 0 for n in edges}
    80	    for targets in edges.values():
    81	        for target in targets:
    82	            indegree[target] += 1
    83	    ready = sorted(n for n, d in indegree.items() if d == 0)
    84	    order: list[str] = []
    85	    while ready:
    86	        n = ready.pop(0)
    87	        order.append(n)
    88	        for target in sorted(edges[n]):
    89	            indegree[target] -= 1
    90	            if indegree[target] == 0:
    91	                ready.append(target)
    92	                ready.sort()
    93	    if len(order) != len(edges):
    94	        stuck = sorted(set(edges) - set(order))
    95	        msg = f"pipeline is not a DAG — cycle through: {', '.join(stuck)}"
    96	        raise LintError(msg)
    97	    return order
    98	
    99	
   100	def _reaches(edges: dict[str, set[str]], src: str, dst: str) -> bool:
   101	    seen: set[str] = set()
   102	    stack = [src]
   103	    while stack:
   104	        n = stack.pop()
   105	        if n == dst:
   106	            return True
   107	        for t in edges.get(n, ()):
   108	            if t not in seen:
   109	                seen.add(t)
   110	                stack.append(t)
   111	    return False
   112	
   113	
   114	def _citation_resolves(citation: str, root: Path) -> bool:
   115	    m = re.match(r"^akar:([\w.-]+)@([0-9a-f]{8,64})$", citation)
   116	    if not m:
   117	        return False
   118	    record_id = m.group(1)
   119	    akar_dir = root / "akar"
   120	    if not akar_dir.is_dir():
   121	        return False
   122	    for f in akar_dir.rglob("*"):
   123	        if f.is_file() and record_id in f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"):
   124	            return True
   125	    return False
   126	
   127	
   128	def lint(season_path: Path) -> list[Finding]:
   129	    findings: list[Finding] = []
   130	    root = _find_root(season_path)
   131	    season = yamlio.load(season_path)
   132	
   133	    def err(msg: str) -> None:
   134	        findings.append(Finding("error", msg, str(season_path)))
   135	
   136	    def warn(msg: str) -> None:
   137	        findings.append(Finding("warning", msg, str(season_path)))
   138	
   139	    # --- required keys
   140	    for key in ("id", "goal", "metric", "mode", "methodology", "benih", "stop"):
   141	        if key not in season:
   142	            err(f"missing required key: {key}")
   143	    if findings:
   144	        return findings
   145	
   146	    if not ID_RE.match(str(season["id"])):
   147	        err(f"id '{season['id']}' does not match s<N>")
   148	    if not str(season["goal"]).strip():
   149	        err("goal is empty")
   150	    if not str(season["metric"]).strip():
   151	        err("metric is empty")
   152	    if season["mode"] not in ("fight", "collab"):
   153	        err(f"mode must be fight|collab, got {season['mode']!r}")
   154	    if season["mode"] == "collab" and "collab" not in season:
   155	        err("mode collab requires a collab: block (lane, share, merge)")
   156	
   157	    # --- autonomy (campaign-level, from rumpun.yaml)
   158	    project = yamlio.load(root / "rumpun.yaml")
   159	    autonomy = project.get("autonomy", {})
   160	    stage = autonomy.get("stage", "manual")
   161	    if stage not in STAGES:
   162	        err(f"rumpun.yaml autonomy.stage must be manual|panel|free, got {stage!r}")
   163	    inv = set(autonomy.get("invariants", []))
   164	    missing = REQUIRED_INVARIANTS - inv
   165	    if missing:
   166	        err(f"rumpun.yaml autonomy.invariants missing {sorted(missing)}")
   167	
   168	    # --- methodology and pipeline
   169	    method = season["methodology"]
   170	    if not isinstance(method, dict) or "approach" not in method:
   171	        err("methodology.approach is required")
   172	    pipeline = method.get("pipeline")
   173	    if not pipeline or not isinstance(pipeline, list):
   174	        err("methodology.pipeline must be a non-empty list of nodes")
   175	        return findings
   176	
   177	    phases = [n.get("phase") for n in pipeline]
   178	    if len(set(phases)) != len(phases):
   179	        err(f"duplicate phase names: {phases}")
   180	
   181	    for node in pipeline:
   182	        phase = node.get("phase", "?")
   183	        if node.get("primitive") not in PRIMITIVES:
   184	            err(f"node '{phase}': primitive must be one of {sorted(PRIMITIVES)}")
   185	        if not node.get("agent") and node.get("agents") != "benih":
   186	            err(f"node '{phase}': needs agent or agents: benih")
   187	        prompt = node.get("prompt")
   188	        if not prompt:
   189	            err(f"node '{phase}': missing prompt")
   190	        elif not (root / prompt).is_file():
   191	            err(f"node '{phase}': prompt not found: {prompt}")
   192	        if "writes" not in node:
   193	            err(f"node '{phase}': missing writes (artifact contract)")
   194	
   195	    try:
   196	        producers = _artifact_producers(pipeline)
   197	        edges = _dag_edges(pipeline, producers)
   198	        _topo_order(edges)
   199	    except LintError as exc:
   200	        err(str(exc))
   201	        return findings
   202	
   203	    # falsify pre-registration: execute must consume the falsify node's output
   204	    phase_prims = {n["phase"]: n.get("primitive") for n in pipeline if "phase" in n}
   205	    falsify_nodes = [p for p, prim in phase_prims.items() if prim == "falsify"]
   206	    execute_nodes = [p for p, prim in phase_prims.items() if prim == "execute"]
   207	    reaches = any(_reaches(edges, f, e) for f in falsify_nodes for e in execute_nodes)
   208	    if falsify_nodes and execute_nodes and not reaches:
   209	        err(
   210	            "falsify does not reach execute: kill criterion is not an input to "
   211	            "execution (must_precede violated)"
   212	        )
   213	
   214	    # --- evidence citations
   215	    evidence = method.get("evidence", [])
   216	    if evidence and not isinstance(evidence, list):
   217	        err("methodology.evidence must be a list")
   218	    for citation in evidence or []:
   219	        if not isinstance(citation, str) or not _citation_resolves(citation, root):
   220	            err(f"evidence citation does not resolve in akar/: {citation!r}")
   221	
   222	    # --- primary_change (P12)
   223	    parent = season.get("parent")
   224	    if parent is not None:
   225	        change = method.get("primary_change")
   226	        if not isinstance(change, dict):
   227	            err("non-seed season requires methodology.primary_change")
   228	        else:
   229	            ctype = change.get("type")
   230	            if ctype not in CHANGE_TYPES | BUNDLE_CLASSES:
   231	                err(f"primary_change.type must be one of {sorted(CHANGE_TYPES | BUNDLE_CLASSES)}")
   232	            if ctype == "free_bundle" and stage not in ("panel", "free"):
   233	                err("free_bundle requires autonomy.stage panel|free")
   234	            for field in ("baseline", "expected_band", "rollback", "eval_window"):
   235	                if not change.get(field):
   236	                    err(f"primary_change.{field} is required: state the band first")
   237	
   238	    # --- benih
   239	    benih = season["benih"]
   240	    if not isinstance(benih, list) or not benih:
   241	        err("benih must be a non-empty list")
   242	    else:
   243	        names = [b.get("name") for b in benih if isinstance(b, dict)]
   244	        if len(set(names)) != len(names):
   245	            err(f"duplicate benih names: {names}")
   246	        for b in benih:
   247	            name = b.get("name", "?")
   248	            if not b.get("route"):
   249	                err(f"benih '{name}': missing route")
   250	            if b.get("knowledge") not in KNOWLEDGE:
   251	                err(f"benih '{name}': knowledge must be one of {sorted(KNOWLEDGE)}")
   252	            minutes = (b.get("budget") or {}).get("minutes")
   253	            if not isinstance(minutes, int) or minutes <= 0:
   254	                err(f"benih '{name}': budget.minutes must be a positive int")
   255	            prompt = b.get("prompt")
   256	            if prompt and not (root / prompt).is_file():
   257	                err(f"benih '{name}': prompt not found: {prompt}")
   258	
   259	    # --- stop rules
   260	    if not season["stop"].get("on"):
   261	        err("stop.on must list at least one rule")
   262	
   263	    # --- warnings
   264	    if stage == "free" and evidence:
   265	        warn("stage is free: audit panel is skipped by design")
   266	    if not evidence and parent is not None:
   267	        warn("non-seed season has no evidence citations (lint passes; panel will not)")
   268	    return findings

     1	"""Agent-route detection: which models can this machine spawn?
     2	
     3	Build order step 2. Port of kancil ``routes.py`` (verified in kancil source,
     4	2026-09-14). Spawn routing hides in three places: which CLI binaries exist,
     5	which env vars point a binary at a proxy (``ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`` + token ->
     6	z.ai-style GLM proxy vs claude.ai login), and per-model flags. ``rumpun
     7	models`` prints the route table; ``--write`` fills the ``routes:`` block of
     8	rumpun.yaml. No function here ever prints or returns a token VALUE — only
     9	var names and endpoints.
    10	
    11	The env-strip prefix (STRIP_ENV) is the known list of vars the claude binary
    12	reads for routing/overrides; extend it when a new override var ships (drift
    13	is visible in the detector output, which prints exactly which vars it saw).
    14	Rumpun adds ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_FABLE_MODEL to kancil's six (tier-default var
    15	observed live in this environment, 2026-09-14).
    16	"""

    19

    22	import os
    23	import re
    24	import shutil
    25	import subprocess

    29	STRIP_ENV = (
    30	    "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL",
    31	    "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN",
    32	    "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
    33	    "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL",

    41	# Well-known proxy hosts -> family label. Unknown hosts fall back to hostname.
    42	_PROXY_HOST_LABELS = {
    43	    "api.z.ai": "glm",
    44	    "api.bigmodel.cn": "glm",
    45	}
    46	
    47	
    48	class RoutesError(Exception):
    49	    pass
    50	
    51	
    52	def _strip_prefix() -> str:
    53	    """The literal ``env -u VAR ...`` prefix for login-route spawns."""
    54	    return "env " + " ".join(f"-u {v}" for v in STRIP_ENV)
    55	
    56	
    57	def proxy_family(base_url: str) -> str:
    58	    """Family label for a proxied claude route (host-derived)."""
    59	    host = (urlparse(base_url).hostname or "").lower()
    60	    for known, label in _PROXY_HOST_LABELS.items():
    61	        if host == known or host.endswith("." + known):
    62	            return label
    63	    return host.split(".")[0] or "proxy"
    64	
    65	
    66	def family_label(cmd: str, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> str:
    67	    """Model-TRUE family label for an agent command.
    68	
    69	    Precedence:
    70	    1. ``--model X`` present -> X (explicit wins: fable, glm-5.2...).
    71	    2. claude in cmd, ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL in the EFFECTIVE env, and the
    72	       strip prefix absent -> proxy label (glm for z.ai).
    73	    3. Non-claude known binary anywhere in the cmd -> its name.
    74	    4. First token basename (custom scripts keep their label).
    75	    """
    76	    env = dict(os.environ if env is None else env)
    77	    lowered = cmd.lower()
    78	    m = re.search(r"(?:--model|-m)[ =]([A-Za-z0-9._-]+)", cmd)
    79	    if m:
    80	        return m.group(1).lower()
    81	    has_claude = re.search(r"\bclaude\b", lowered) is not None
    82	    stripped = " -u anthropic_base_url" in lowered
    83	    if has_claude and env.get("ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL") and not stripped:
    84	        return proxy_family(env["ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"])
    85	    for name in KNOWN_CLIS:
    86	        if re.search(rf"\b{name}\b", lowered):
    87	            return name
    88	    token = cmd.strip().split()[0] if cmd.strip() else ""
    89	    return token.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].lower()
    90	
    91	
    92	def detect_clis(timeout: float = 5.0) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    93	    """Which agent CLIs are on PATH, with versions when cheap."""
    94	    rows = []
    95	    for name in KNOWN_CLIS:
    96	        path = shutil.which(name)
    97	        if not path:
    98	            rows.append({"name": name, "present": "no", "version": ""})
    99	            continue
   100	        version = ""
   101	        try:
   102	            out = subprocess.run(
   103	                [path, "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True,
   104	                timeout=timeout, check=False,
   105	            )
   106	            version = (out.stdout or out.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[
   107	                0][:40]
   108	        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   109	            version = ""
   110	        rows.append({
   111	            "name": name, "present": "yes",
   112	            "version": version or "present (version n/a)",
   113	        })
   114	    return rows
   115	
   116	
   117	def codex_model_routes() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   118	    """Spawn-ready routes for every model the codex login exposes.
   119	
   120	    Reads ``~/.codex/models_cache.json`` (refreshed by the codex CLI at
   121	    login/use) and emits one ``codex exec -m <model>`` template per slug
   122	    (gpt-6-astra, gpt-5.6-sol, ...). Falls back to the default codex route
   123	    when no cache exists.
   124	    """
   125	    base = "codex exec --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox"
   126	    cache = Path.home() / ".codex" / "models_cache.json"
   127	    slugs: list[str] = []
   128	    with contextlib.suppress(OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
   129	        data = json.loads(cache.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   130	        raw = data.get("models") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
   131	        for item in raw or []:
   132	            slug = item.get("slug") or item.get("id")
   133	            if slug:
   134	                slugs.append(str(slug))
   135	    if not slugs:
   136	        return [{
   137	            "family": "codex",
   138	            "desc": "default codex model",
   139	            "command": f"{base} {{prompt}}",
   140	        }]
   141	    return [
   142	        {
   143	            "family": slug.lower(),
   144	            "desc": f"codex model {slug}",
   145	            "command": f"{base} -m {slug} {{prompt}}",
   146	        }
   147	        for slug in slugs
   148	    ]
   149	
   150	
   151	def claude_routes(env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   152	    """Every claude-binary route on this machine.
   153	
   154	    Returns spawn-ready dicts: family label, one-line description, and the
   155	    exact spawn command template. The env-strip route is ALWAYS listed when
   156	    a claude binary exists — it is how the claude.ai login is reached from
   157	    a proxied shell.
   158	    """
   159	    env = dict(os.environ if env is None else env)
   160	    routes: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   161	    base = env.get("ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL", "")
   162	    tiers = {
   163	        tier: env[f"ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_{tier.upper()}_MODEL"]
   164	        for tier in ("sonnet", "opus", "haiku", "fable")
   165	        if env.get(f"ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_{tier.upper()}_MODEL")
   166	    }
   167	    if base:
   168	        fam = proxy_family(base)
   169	        desc = f"proxy {base}"
   170	        if tiers:
   171	            desc += " — tiers: " + ", ".join(f"{t}={m}" for t, m in tiers.items())
   172	        routes.append({
   173	            "family": fam,
   174	            "desc": desc,
   175	            "command": "cat {prompt} | claude -p --dangerously-skip-permissions",
   176	            "env_note": f"inherited env (base_url + {len(tiers)} tier default var(s) set)",
   177	        })
   178	    login_desc = "claude.ai login (subscription) via env-strip"
   179	    tier_note = ""
   180	    if tiers:
   181	        tier_note = " + --model <name> (tier defaults are ALSO env-pinned and must be stripped)"
   182	    routes.append({
   183	        "family": "claude",
   184	        "desc": login_desc + (tier_note if tiers else ""),
   185	        "command": (
   186	            f"{_strip_prefix()} sh -c 'cat {{prompt}} | claude -p "
   187	            "--dangerously-skip-permissions --model <model>'"
   188	        ),
   189	        "env_note": f"strip prefix removes {len(STRIP_ENV)} routing vars",
   190	    })
   191	    return routes
   192	
   193	
   194	def route_table() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   195	    """Combined spawn-ready route list (claude-binary routes, then codex)."""
   196	    table = list(claude_routes())
   197	    table.extend(codex_model_routes())
   198	    return table
   199	
   200	
   201	def write_routes(cfg_path: Path) -> int:
   202	    """Fill the ``routes: {}`` placeholder line of rumpun.yaml.
   203	
   204	    Text-level single-line patch so scaffold comments survive. Raises
   205	    RoutesError when the placeholder is missing (already filled, or not a
   206	    scaffolded config).
   207	    """
   208	    text = cfg_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   209	    table = route_table()
   210	    out: list[str] = []
   211	    patched = False
   212	    for line in text.splitlines():
   213	        if re.match(r"^routes:\s*\{\}\s*(#.*)?$", line):
   214	            out.append("routes:")
   215	            for route in table:
   216	                cmd = route["command"].replace("'", "''")
   217	                out.append(f"  {route['family']}: '{cmd}'")
   218	            patched = True
   219	        else:
   220	            out.append(line)
   221	    if not patched:
   222	        msg = "rumpun.yaml has no empty `routes: {}` line to fill (already written?)"
   223	        raise RoutesError(msg)
   224	    cfg_path.write_text("\n".join(out) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   225	    return len(table)

   108	PROMPTS = {
   109	    "analyze": """\
   110	# Phase: analyze (error analysis on current best output)
   111	
   112	## Inputs
   113	- current best output + metric record (see season YAML `metric`)
   114	
   115	## Task
   116	- Enumerate concrete errors/gaps in the current best output. One error per line.
   117	- For each: where it shows, evidence (file/line/metric delta), and a severity guess.
   118	
   119	## Output contract
   120	- Write `errors.jsonl`: one JSON object per error:
   121	  {"id": "e1", "where": "...", "evidence": "...", "severity": "high|med|low"}
   122	
   123	## Constraints
   124	- Every claim cites something on disk. No invented numbers.
   125	- Do not propose solutions here; that is the hypothesize phase.
   126	""",
   127	    "rank_gaps": """\
   128	# Phase: rank_gaps (pick the top points worth resolving)
   129	
   130	## Inputs
   131	- `errors.jsonl` from analyze.
   132	
   133	## Task
   134	- Choose the top `top_n` gaps by expected metric impact over effort.
   135	- Discard duplicates and cosmetic items explicitly (name what you dropped and why).
   136	
   137	## Output contract
   138	- Write `gaps.yaml`: list of {id, summary, source_error_ids, expected_impact, effort}.
   139	
   140	## Constraints
   141	- Do not invent new errors; rank what analyze produced.
   142	""",
   143	    "hypothesize": """\
   144	# Phase: hypothesize (one hypothesis per gap)
   145	
   146	## Inputs
   147	- `gaps.yaml`.
   148	
   149	## Task
   150	- One hypothesis per gap: a falsifiable statement of cause and expected effect.
   151	- State the mechanism, not just the correlation.
   152	
   153	## Output contract
   154	- Write `hypotheses.yaml`: {gap_id, hypothesis, mechanism, expected_direction}.
   155	
   156	## Constraints
   157	- A hypothesis the falsify phase cannot kill is not a hypothesis; sharpen it.
   158	""",
   159	    "plan": """\
   160	# Phase: plan (experiment that tests each hypothesis)
   161	
   162	## Inputs
   163	- `hypotheses.yaml`.
   164	
   165	## Task
   166	- One experiment per hypothesis: what runs, on what data, measured how.
   167	- Cheapest experiment that can kill the hypothesis first.
   168	
   169	## Output contract
   170	- Write `experiments.yaml`: {hypothesis_id, command/steps, data, metric_readout}.
   171	
   172	## Constraints
   173	- Experiments must run inside this rimba workspace. No network calls outside declared routes.
   174	""",
   175	    "falsify": """\
   176	# Phase: falsify (pre-register the kill criterion BEFORE execution)
   177	
   178	## Inputs
   179	- `experiments.yaml`.
   180	
   181	## Task
   182	- For each experiment, write the kill criterion BEFORE results exist (P16, structured):
   183	  comparator, metric, direction, threshold, dataset, confidence rule.
   184	- State the consequence: kill | revise | retain | inconclusive.
   185	
   186	## Output contract
   187	- Write `falsification.yaml`: {experiment_id, metric, direction, threshold, dataset,
   188	  confidence_rule, consequence}.
   189	
   190	## Constraints
   191	- This file is sealed (hashed) before execute starts. It cannot be edited after results.
   192	- A criterion of the form "it works" is invalid; give a number and a direction.
   193	""",
   194	    "execute": """\
   195	# Phase: execute (run the experiments as the season's benih agents)
   196	
   197	## Inputs
   198	- `falsification.yaml` (sealed criteria).
   199	- Your lane directive and knowledge staging from akar.
   200	
   201	## Task
   202	- Run your assigned experiments. Append discoveries to your discovery.md as you go.
   203	- Fight mode: work independently. Collab mode: post leads to the lane (leads, not facts).
   204	
   205	## Output contract
   206	- Write `results.jsonl`: {experiment_id, outcome numbers, artifacts produced, minutes}.
   207	
   208	## Constraints
   209	- Do not read or reinterpret the falsification criteria; execute them as written.
   210	""",
   211	    "evaluate": """\
   212	# Phase: evaluate (results vs committed falsification criteria)
   213	
   214	## Inputs
   215	- `falsification.yaml` (sealed), `results.jsonl`.
   216	
   217	## Task
   218	- Quote each committed criterion verbatim, then the result, then the verdict:
   219	  kill | revise | retain | inconclusive (P16).
   220	- Season verdict per benih: WIN | LOSS | NEUTRAL | INVALID, with an `implies` line.
   221	
   222	## Output contract
   223	- Write `verdicts.jsonl`: {experiment_id, criterion_verbatim, result, verdict, implies}.
   224	
   225	## Constraints
   226	- WIN below the declared noise floor is recorded as NEUTRAL with the reason (P1/P13).
   227	- Every verdict cites on-disk evidence. An empty implies line is a defect, not a style.
   228	""",
   229	}

   231	README = """\
   232	# rumpun project state (this directory: .rumpun/)
   233	
   234	Everything the swarm needs lives here, out of the host repo's way:
   235	- rumpun.yaml — campaign config: goal, autonomy stages, budget cap, routes.
   236	- musim/ — one YAML per season; s1 is the seed.
   237	- akar/ — append-only evidence ledger (committed).
   238	- prompts/base/ — phase prompt templates; season overrides in prompts/sN/.
   239	- rimba/ — per-season workspaces (gitignored).
   240	
   241	This directory is COMMITTED except rimba/: git history over it is the
   242	evolution ledger. Unlike .omc-style caches it is not disposable.
   243	
   244	## First season
   245	1. Fill campaign.goal and campaign.metric in rumpun.yaml.
   246	2. Fill goal/metric in musim/s1.yaml.
   247	3. rumpun lint .rumpun/musim/s1.yaml
   248	4. rumpun graph .rumpun/musim/s1.yaml
   249	5. rumpun models — probe spawnable routes; --write fills rumpun.yaml.
   250	6. rumpun season start — run the season (not yet implemented, build order step 3).
   251	"""

   253	
   254	def _write(path: Path, content: str, overwrite: bool = False) -> None:
   255	    if path.exists() and not overwrite:
   256	        msg = f"refusing to overwrite existing file: {path}"
   257	        raise ScaffoldError(msg)
   258	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   259	    path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
   260	    logger.info("wrote %s", path)
   261	
   262	
   263	def init_project(target: Path) -> None:
   264	    if (target / RUMPUN_DIR / "rumpun.yaml").exists():
   265	        msg = f"{target} already has .rumpun/rumpun.yaml — init refuses to overwrite"
   266	        raise ScaffoldError(msg)
   267	    _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "rumpun.yaml", RUMPUN_YAML)
   268	    _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "musim" / "s1.yaml", MUSIM_S1)
   269	    _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "musim" / "_template.yaml", MUSIM_TEMPLATE)
   270	    for phase, content in PROMPTS.items():
   271	        _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "prompts" / "base" / f"{phase}.md", content)
   272	    _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "README.md", README)
   273	    for keep in (f"{RUMPUN_DIR}/akar/.gitkeep",):
   274	        _write(target / keep, "")
   275	    _write(target / RUMPUN_DIR / "rimba" / ".gitignore", "*\n!.gitignore\n")

    90	autonomy:

    92	  invariants: [goal_immutable, budget_cap, falsify_required]
    93	budget:
    94	  campaign_cost_cap: 100
    95	routes:
    96	  glm: "cat {prompt} | true"
    97	"""
    98	
    99	SEASON_S1 = """\
   100	id: s1
   101	goal: "fixture"
   102	metric: "m"
   103	mode: fight
   104	methodology:
   105	  approach: "x"
   106	  evidence: []
   107	  primary_change:
   108	    type: add
   109	    node: execute
   110	    baseline: "b"
   111	    expected_band: "WIN if x"
   112	    rollback: "git revert"
   113	    eval_window: "s1"
   114	  pipeline:
   115	    - phase: execute
   116	      primitive: execute
   117	      agents: benih
   118	      prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   119	      writes: results.jsonl
   120	benih:
   121	  - name: w1
   122	    route: glm
   123	    prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   124	    knowledge: none
   125	    budget: {minutes: 1}
   126	stop:
   127	  "on": [all_exited]
   128	"""
   129	
   130	# Same shape as SEASON_S1 but with two benih, so "each name exactly once"
   131	# in the spawned map has real content to check against.
   132	SEASON_DUAL = """\
   133	id: s1
   134	goal: "fixture"
   135	metric: "m"
   136	mode: fight
   137	methodology:
   138	  approach: "x"
   139	  evidence: []
   140	  primary_change:
   141	    type: add
   142	    node: execute
   143	    baseline: "b"
   144	    expected_band: "WIN if x"
   145	    rollback: "git revert"
   146	    eval_window: "s1"
   147	  pipeline:
   148	    - phase: execute
   149	      primitive: execute
   150	      agents: benih
   151	      prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   152	      writes: results.jsonl
   153	benih:
   154	  - name: alpha
   155	    route: glm
   156	    prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   157	    knowledge: none
   158	    budget: {minutes: 1}
   159	  - name: beta
   160	    route: glm
   161	    prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   162	    knowledge: none
   163	    budget: {minutes: 1}
   164	stop:
   165	  "on": [all_exited]
   166	"""
   167	
   168	
   169	def _write_proj(tmp_path, season_text, season_name="s1.yaml", project_yaml=RUMPUN_YAML):
   170	    """Build <tmp>/proj/.rumpun: rumpun.yaml, one season, the dummy prompt."""
   171	    root = tmp_path / "proj" / ".rumpun"
   172	    (root / "musim").mkdir(parents=True)
   173	    (root / "prompts" / "dev").mkdir(parents=True)
   174	    (root / "rumpun.yaml").write_text(project_yaml, encoding="utf-8")
   175	    (root / "prompts" / "dev" / "dummy.md").write_text("prompt body\n", encoding="utf-8")
   176	    season = root / "musim" / season_name
   177	    season.write_text(season_text, encoding="utf-8")
   178	    return root, season
   179	
   180	
   181	def _agent_ws(tmp_path):
   182	    """One agent workspace whose state.json proc_start never matches a live pid."""
   183	    ws = tmp_path / "a"
   184	    ws.mkdir()
   185	    (ws / "state.json").write_text(
   186	        json.dumps(
   187	            {
   188	                "name": "a",
   189	                "route": "glm",
   190	                "cmd": "x",
   191	                "pid": 1,
   192	                "proc_start": 987654321,
   193	                "started_at": 0.0,
   194	            }
   195	        ),
   196	        encoding="utf-8",
   197	    )
   198	    return ws
   199

   221	    ws = _agent_ws(tmp_path)

   241	    path = akar.append_record(tmp_path, "rec-1", "title one", "body text")

   251	        akar.append_record(tmp_path, "rec-1", "second try", "other body")

   336	    assert Path(lane["lock"]).is_file()

   410	# --- lane concurrency (w2 s4 scope, harness-applied) --------------------------
   411	
   412	
   413	def test_collab_append_event_concurrent_dense_seq(tmp_path):
   414	    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
   415	
   416	    lane = collab.prepare_lane(tmp_path, "s1", "dev")
   417	
   418	    def burst(k):
   419	        for i in range(25):
   420	            collab.append_event(lane, f"w{k}", {"i": i})
   421	
   422	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
   423	        list(pool.map(burst, range(4)))
   424	    seqs = [e["seq"] for e in collab.read_events(lane)]
   425	    assert seqs == list(range(100))
   426

   428	# --- state lock (w1 s5 scope; concurrency test harness-applied) ---------------
   429	
   430	
   431	def test_save_state_lock_serializes_concurrent_writers(tmp_path):
   432	    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
   433	
   434	    root = tmp_path
   435	    (root / "rimba" / "s1" / "_season").mkdir(parents=True)
   436	
   437	    def write(k):
   438	        for i in range(10):
   439	            engine._save_state(root, "s1", {"n": k * 10 + i})
   440	
   441	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
   442	        list(pool.map(write, range(4)))
   443	    state = engine._load_state(root, "s1")
   444	    assert isinstance(state.get("n"), int)
   445	    assert (root / "rimba" / "s1" / "_season" / "state.lock").is_file()
   446	
   447	
   448	# --- dual start at process level (w2 s6 scope) --------------------------------
   449	
   450	
   451	def test_dual_start_single_spawner(tmp_path):
   452	    """Two concurrent `rumpun season start` processes spawn each agent once.
   453	
   454	    Starter 1 holds _season/state.lock across load, the finished check, the
   455	    initial save, and the whole spawn loop. Starter 2 blocks on the flock,
   456	    then reads the fully spawned state and reattaches (both names already in
   457	    `spawned` -> continue) instead of spawning again. Proven by: both exit 0,
   458	    the season reaches exactly one terminal state.json with status
   459	    completed, each benih name appears in the spawned map exactly once, both
   460	    per-agent workspaces hold a state.json, and the "spawned alpha" log line
   461	    appears in exactly one of the two stderr streams.
   462	    """
   463	    root, season = _write_proj(tmp_path, SEASON_DUAL, project_yaml=RUMPUN_YAML_DUAL)
   464	    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "rumpun", "season", "start", str(season)]
   465	    # Both Popens go out back-to-back so starter 2 reaches the flock while
   466	    # starter 1 still holds it; its state read then happens under the lock.
   467	    starters = [
   468	        subprocess.Popen(
   469	            cmd,
   470	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   471	            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   472	            text=True,
   473	            cwd=str(tmp_path),
   474	        )
   475	        for _ in range(2)
   476	    ]
   477	    results = []
   478	    for starter in starters:
   479	        try:
   480	            results.append(starter.communicate(timeout=180))
   481	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   482	            starter.kill()
   483	            out, err = starter.communicate()
   484	            pytest.fail(f"season start did not finish in 180s; stdout={out} stderr={err}")
   485	    for starter, (_out, err) in zip(starters, results, strict=True):
   486	        assert starter.returncode == 0, f"starter exited badly; stderr: {err}"
   487	    spawn_logs = sum(err.count("spawned alpha") for _out, err in results)
   488	    assert spawn_logs == 1, "alpha was spawned twice: state.lock did not serialize"
   489	    final = root / "rimba" / "s1" / "_season" / "state.json"
   490	    state = json.loads(final.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   491	    assert state["status"] == "completed"
   492	    spawned = list(state["spawned"])
   493	    assert spawned.count("alpha") == 1
   494	    assert spawned.count("beta") == 1
   495	    assert len(spawned) == 2
   496	    for name in ("alpha", "beta"):
   497	        assert (root / "rimba" / "s1" / name / "state.json").is_file()
   498	
   499	
   500	def test_collab_append_event_first_use_creates_files(tmp_path):
   501	    lane = {
   502	        "file": str(tmp_path / "fresh" / "lane-x.jsonl"),

   505	    event = collab.append_event(lane, "operator", {"text": "hi"})
   506	    assert event["seq"] == 0
   507	    assert len(collab.read_events(lane)) == 1

   599	# --- audit reflection (w2 s11 scope, spec-first) ---------------------------------
   600	
   601	
   602	AUDIT_SEASON = """\
   603	id: {sid}
   604	goal: "fixture"
   605	metric: "m"
   606	mode: fight
   607	methodology:
   608	  approach: "x"
   609	  evidence: []
   610	  primary_change:
   611	    type: add
   612	    node: execute
   613	    baseline: "b"
   614	    expected_band: "WIN if x"
   615	    rollback: "git revert"
   616	    eval_window: "{sid}"
   617	  pipeline:
   618	    - phase: execute
   619	      primitive: execute
   620	      agents: benih
   621	      prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   622	      writes: results.jsonl
   623	    - phase: evaluate
   624	      primitive: evaluate
   625	      agents: benih
   626	      prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   627	      writes: verdicts.jsonl
   628	benih:
   629	  - name: w1
   630	    route: glm
   631	    prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md
   632	    knowledge: none
   633	    budget: {{minutes: 1}}
   634	stop:
   635	  "on": [all_exited]
   636	"""
   637	
   638	
   639	def _write_audit_proj(tmp_path, sids=("s1", "s2"), project_yaml=RUMPUN_YAML):
   640	    """Audit fixture root: given rumpun.yaml, N seasons, empty akar/."""
   641	    root = tmp_path / "proj" / ".rumpun"
   642	    (root / "musim").mkdir(parents=True)
   643	    (root / "akar").mkdir()
   644	    (root / "prompts" / "dev").mkdir(parents=True)
   645	    (root / "rumpun.yaml").write_text(project_yaml, encoding="utf-8")
   646	    (root / "prompts" / "dev" / "dummy.md").write_text("prompt body\n", encoding="utf-8")
   647	    for sid in sids:
   648	        (root / "musim" / f"{sid}.yaml").write_text(
   649	            AUDIT_SEASON.format(sid=sid), encoding="utf-8"
   650	        )
   651	    return root
   652	
   653	
   654	def _write_rimba_season(root, sid, status="completed", artifacts=True):
   655	    """One rimba/<sid>: both pipeline artifacts plus _season/state.json."""
   656	    season = root / "rimba" / sid
   657	    season.mkdir(parents=True)
   658	    if artifacts:
   659	        (season / "results.jsonl").write_text('{"n": 1}\n', encoding="utf-8")
   660	        (season / "verdicts.jsonl").write_text('{"v": "WIN"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
   661	    state = season / "_season"
   662	    state.mkdir()
   663	    (state / "state.json").write_text(
   664	        json.dumps({"id": sid, "status": status, "started_at": 1.0, "ended_at": 2.0}),
   665	        encoding="utf-8",
   666	    )
   667	    return season
   668	
   669	
   670	def _write_spawn(root, sid, name, route, outcome):
   671	    """One agent workspace rimba/<sid>/<name>/ shaped to land in `outcome`.
   672	
   673	    outcome is one of clean-deliverable, clean-empty, failed, not-exited
   674	    (terminated marker, no exit file), or crashed (no exit file at all);
   675	    not-exited and crashed are both the spec's "other state" class. The
   676	    __pycache__/mod.pyc and terminated.tmp noise on clean-empty and the
   677	    nested results/findings.md on clean-deliverable pin the recursive
   678	    non-bookkeeping scan: the pyc must not count as a deliverable, the
   679	    nested file must.
   680	    """
   681	    ws = root / "rimba" / sid / name
   682	    ws.mkdir(parents=True)
   683	    for book in ("agent.log", "prompt.md", "prompt-meta.yaml"):
   684	        (ws / book).write_text("book\n", encoding="utf-8")
   685	    (ws / "state.json").write_text(
   686	        json.dumps(
   687	            {
   688	                "name": name,
   689	                "route": route,
   690	                "cmd": "x",
   691	                "pid": 1,
   692	                "proc_start": 987654321,
   693	                "started_at": 0.0,
   694	            }
   695	        ),
   696	        encoding="utf-8",
   697	    )
   698	    # The engine publishes the finalized snap into the season-level
   699	    # _season/state.json agents block; mirror that so the audit's route
   700	    # scan sees the spawn (fields the classifier reads: route, state,
   701	    # exit_code).
   702	    season_state_path = root / "rimba" / sid / "_season" / "state.json"
   703	    season_state = json.loads(season_state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   704	    snap: dict = {"route": route}
   705	    if outcome.startswith("clean"):
   706	        snap.update(state="exited", exit_code=0)
   707	    elif outcome == "failed":
   708	        snap.update(state="failed", exit_code=3)
   709	    elif outcome == "not-exited":
   710	        snap.update(state="terminated")
   711	    else:  # crashed: no exit file, no marker
   712	        snap.update(state="crashed")
   713	    season_state.setdefault("agents", {})[name] = snap
   714	    season_state_path.write_text(json.dumps(season_state), encoding="utf-8")
   715	    if outcome == "not-exited":
   716	        (ws / "terminated").write_text("terminated\n", encoding="utf-8")
   717	    elif outcome != "crashed":
   718	        code = "0" if outcome.startswith("clean") else "3"
   719	        (ws / "exit").write_text(code, encoding="utf-8")
   720	    if outcome == "clean-deliverable":
   721	        nested = ws / "results" / "findings.md"
   722	        nested.parent.mkdir()
   723	        nested.write_text("payload\n", encoding="utf-8")
   724	    if outcome == "clean-empty":
   725	        cache = ws / "__pycache__"
   726	        cache.mkdir()
   727	        (cache / "mod.cpython-310.pyc").write_bytes(b"\x00")
   728	        (ws / "terminated.tmp").write_text("term\n", encoding="utf-8")
   729	    return ws
   730	
   731	
   732	def _write_results_rows(root, sid, rows):
   733	    """Overwrite rimba/<sid>/results.jsonl with the given row dicts."""
   734	    path = root / "rimba" / sid / "results.jsonl"
   735	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   736	    path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(row) + "\n" for row in rows), encoding="utf-8")
   737	    return path
   738	
   739	
   740	def _write_harvest_record(root, sid, verdict):
   741	    """A season-level verdicts.jsonl row — the verdict rule the audit reads."""
   742	    path = root / "rimba" / sid / "verdicts.jsonl"
   743	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   744	    row = {
   745	        "season": sid,
   746	        "verdict": verdict,
   747	        "metric": "modules_integrated",
   748	        "implies": "fixture",
   749	    }
   750	    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   751	        fh.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")
   752	    return path
   753	
   754	
   755	def _audit_base(tmp_path):
   756	    """Base audit fixture: s1 completed with both artifacts, rimba/s2 empty."""
   757	    root = _write_audit_proj(tmp_path)
   758	    _write_rimba_season(root, "s1")
   759	    (root / "rimba" / "s2").mkdir(parents=True)
   760	    return root
   761	
   762	
   763	def _tree_snapshot(root, sub):
   764	    """Map every file under root/<sub> to its bytes; {} when the dir is absent."""
   765	    base = root / sub
   766	    if not base.is_dir():
   767	        return {}
   768	    return {
   769	        str(p.relative_to(base)): p.read_bytes()
   770	        for p in sorted(base.rglob("*"))
   771	        if p.is_file()
   772	    }
   773	
   774	
   775	def test_run_audit_first_record_is_audit_1(tmp_path):
   776	    root = _audit_base(tmp_path)
   777	    record = audit.run_audit(root)
   778	    assert record.parent == root / "akar"
   779	    assert "audit-1" in record.name
   780	    assert "audit-1" in record.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

   786	    second = audit.run_audit(root)
   787	    assert "audit-2" in second.name
   788	    assert "audit-2" in second.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   789	    assert first.is_file()  # append-only: the earlier record survives
   790

   868	def test_audit_route_outcome_lines_per_route(tmp_path):
   869	    root = _write_audit_proj(tmp_path)
   870	    _write_rimba_season(root, "s1")
   871	    _write_rimba_season(root, "s2")
   872	    _write_spawn(root, "s1", "a1", "glm", "clean-deliverable")
   873	    _write_spawn(root, "s1", "a2", "glm", "clean-empty")
   874	    # a2 also carries a terminated marker: exit 0 wins the class and the
   875	    # marker is bookkeeping, so the spawn stays clean-empty.
   876	    ws_a2 = root / "rimba" / "s1" / "a2"
   877	    (ws_a2 / "terminated").write_text("terminated\n", encoding="utf-8")
   878	    _write_spawn(root, "s2", "a3", "glm", "failed")
   879	    _write_spawn(root, "s2", "a4", "glm", "not-exited")
   880	    _write_spawn(root, "s2", "a5", "glm", "crashed")
   881	    _write_spawn(root, "s2", "a6", "fable", "clean-deliverable")
   882	    text = audit.run_audit(root).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   883	    lines = text.splitlines()
   884	    glm = [line for line in lines if "route glm:" in line]
   885	    fable = [line for line in lines if "route fable:" in line]
   886	    assert len(glm) == 1
   887	    assert len(fable) == 1
   888	    want_glm = (
   889	        "route glm: 1 clean-deliverable, 1 clean-empty, 1 failed, "
   890	        "2 not-exited over 5 spawns"
   891	    )
   892	    assert want_glm in glm[0]
   893	    assert "s1" in glm[0] and "s2" in glm[0]  # the line cites its seasons
   894	    want_fable = (
   895	        "route fable: 1 clean-deliverable, 0 clean-empty, 0 failed, "
   896	        "0 not-exited over 1 spawns"
   897	    )
   898	    assert want_fable in fable[0]
   899	    assert "s2" in fable[0] and "s1" not in fable[0]  # only its own evidence
   900	    assert "tool-check" not in text  # one clean-empty is below the trigger
   901	
   902

   975	    root = _write_audit_proj(tmp_path)

   996	            "      agents: benih\n"
   997	            "      prompt: prompts/dev/dummy.md\n"
   998	            "      writes: reflections.jsonl\n",
   999	        ),
  1000	        encoding="utf-8",
  1001	    )

  1011	    assert "recalibrate LOSS bands" in candidates[2]

  1013	    assert not any("campaign_cost_cap" in line for line in candidates)  # ...never proposed

  1046	def test_report_workspace_links(tmp_path):

  1120	def test_audit_f4_route_counts_unchanged_by_toolless_mark(tmp_path):
  1121	    """run_audit's F4 outcome counts ignore the additive toolless mark.
  1122	
  1123	    Same fixture, two audit runs, the only delta being toolless: true
  1124	    stamped onto both finalized snaps between them: the per-route line
  1125	    must come out identical.
  1126	    """
  1127	    root = _write_audit_proj(tmp_path)
  1128	    _write_rimba_season(root, "s1")

  1131	    _write_spawn(root, "s2", "t2", "glm", "clean-empty")

  1133	        "route glm: 1 clean-deliverable, 1 clean-empty, 0 failed, "
  1134	        "0 not-exited over 2 spawns"
  1135	    )

  1141	        state["agents"][name]["toolless"] = True
  1142	        path.write_text(json.dumps(state), encoding="utf-8")
  1143	    after = audit.run_audit(root).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1144	    glm_before = [ln for ln in before.splitlines() if "route glm:" in ln]
  1145	    glm_after = [ln for ln in after.splitlines() if "route glm:" in ln]

  1150	# --- stall semantics (harness hot-fix after s15: runtime killed live spawns) ----
  1151	
  1152	
  1153	def test_agent_snap_stall_tracks_log_progress_not_runtime(tmp_path):
  1154	    """A spawn streaming into agent.log is running however long it runs.
  1155	
  1156	    s15 regression: stalled was computed from started_at alone, so any
  1157	    spawn alive past stall_minutes was killed mid-work. Durable progress
  1158	    is agent.log growth (mtime); a frozen log past the window stalls.
  1159	    """
  1160	    ws = tmp_path / "w1"
  1161	    ws.mkdir()
  1162	    meta = {
  1163	        "name": "w1",
  1164	        "route": "glm",
  1165	        "pid": os.getpid(),
  1166	        "proc_start": engine._proc_start_ticks(os.getpid()),
  1167	        "started_at": time.time() - 60.0,
  1168	    }
  1169	    (ws / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta), encoding="utf-8")
  1170	    log = ws / "agent.log"
  1171	    log.write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
  1172	    assert engine._agent_snap(ws, 1.0)["state"] == "running"
  1173	    old = time.time() - 30.0
  1174	    os.utime(log, (old, old))
  1175	    assert engine._agent_snap(ws, 1.0)["state"] == "stalled"
   413	    # Candidates: deterministic triggers only, phases in pipeline order
   414	    # first, then route, then calibration, stall trigger last, hard cap.
   415	    # Never invented, never over cap.
   416	    proposals: list[tuple[str, str]] = []  # (short label, record line)
   417	    if n >= 2:
   418	        for phase, writes, k in liveness:
   419	            if k == 0:
   420	                proposals.append(
   421	                    (
   422	                        f"{phase} ({writes})",
   423	                        f"candidate: exercise or trim phase {phase} — {writes} "
   424	                        f"written in 0 of {n} engine seasons ({ids_str}); "
   425	                        f"band: WIN when a later season's ledger holds {writes} "
   426	                        f"or the pipeline drops {phase}",
   427	                    )
   428	                )
   429	    for route in sorted(route_counts):
   430	        empty = route_counts[route]["clean-empty"]
   431	        if empty >= ROUTED_EMPTY_TRIGGER:
   432	            proposals.append(
   433	                (
   434	                    f"route {route} ({empty} clean-empty)",
   435	                    f"candidate: route {route}: {empty} clean-empty spawns — add spawn "
   436	                    f"tool-check or prefer alternative route ({','.join(route_sids[route])}); "
   437	                    f"band: WIN when clean-empty rate drops to 0 or a tool-check lands",
   438	                )
   439	            )
   440	    if len(masked) >= BAND_MASKED_SEASONS:
   441	        proposals.append(
   442	            (
   443	                "LOSS band calibration",
   444	                f"candidate: recalibrate LOSS bands — count integrated deliverables in "
   445	                f"the band ({','.join(sid for sid, _k in masked)}); "
   446	                f"band: WIN when no later LOSS season ships an integrated module "
   447	                f"its band ignores",
   448	            )
   449	        )
   450	    if len(stalled) >= STALL_RECURRENCE:
   451	        proposals.append(
   452	            (
   453	                "stall/budget rules",
   454	                f"candidate: re-size stall/budget rules — stopped_stall in "
   455	                f"{len(stalled)} seasons ({','.join(stalled)}); "
   456	                f"band: WIN when a later season reaches a terminal state "
   457	                f"without stopped_stall",
   458	            )
   459	        )
   460	    dropped = [label for label, _line in proposals[MAX_CANDIDATES:]]
   461	    candidates = [line for _label, line in proposals[:MAX_CANDIDATES]]
   462	    if not candidates:
   463	        lines.append("candidates: none — no trigger met; nothing proposed without evidence")
   464	    lines.extend(candidates)
   465	    if dropped:
   466	        lines.append(
   467	            f"candidate cap {MAX_CANDIDATES} reached in pipeline order; "
   468	            f"also triggered but unproposed here: {','.join(dropped)}"
   469	        )
   470	    return lines, candidates
   471	
   472	
   473	def run_audit(root: Path, last_n: int = 10) -> Path:
   474	    """Reflect over the last `last_n` seasons; append the audit akar record.
   475	
   476	    Reads musim/s*.yaml (sorted by number, last N), the ledger artifacts
   477	    under rimba/<sid>/, the latest season's declared pipeline, per-season
   478	    state.json statuses and finalized agent snapshots, verdicts.jsonl and
   479	    results.jsonl rows, and the rumpun.yaml budget block. Every claim the
   480	    record makes cites its season ids; the only mutation is the appended
   481	    record (id audit-<k>, k = 1 + existing audit-* records by akar/
   482	    filename scan). akar.AkarError wraps into AuditError. Returns the
   483	    record path.
   484	    """
   485	    if last_n < 1:
   486	        raise AuditError(f"last_n must be >= 1, got {last_n}")
   487	    seasons = _seasons(root, last_n)
   488	    engine_ids = [sid for sid, _p in seasons if (root / "rimba" / sid).is_dir()]
   489	    if not engine_ids:
   490	        raise AuditError(
   491	            f"no audited season has a rimba/ directory under {root}; "
   492	            "nothing to reflect on"
   493	        )
   494	    body, _candidates = _findings_and_candidates(root, seasons)
   495	    record_id = f"audit-{_next_index(root)}"
   496	    try:
   497	        path = akar.append_record(root, record_id, "reflection audit", "\n".join(body))
   498	    except akar.AkarError as exc:
   499	        raise AuditError(f"cannot append audit record {record_id}: {exc}") from exc
   500	    return path
   501	
   502	
   503	def candidate_lines(record: Path) -> list[str]:
   504	    """The record's candidate lines, for the verb's own output.
   505	
   506	    Body recovery follows the akar layout docstring: body lines are
   507	    lines[4:-1] between the header and the sha256 trailer.
   508	    """
   509	    try:
   510	        lines = record.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
   511	    except OSError as exc:
   512	        logger.exception("cannot read audit record %s", record)
   513	        raise AuditError(f"cannot read {record}: {exc}") from exc
   514	    return [line for line in lines[4:-1] if line.startswith("candidate:")]

import runpy,tempfile,threading,os,json,subprocess,builtins
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from unittest.mock import patch
from rumpun import akar,engine,lint,routes,report,evolve
f=runpy.run_path('tests/test_rumpun.py')
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rumpun-review-') as td:
    p=Path(td)
    root,season=f['_write_proj'](p/'names',f['SEASON_S1'])
    for name in ['_season','../../outside']:
        season.write_text(f['SEASON_S1'].replace('name: w1',f'name: {name}'))
        print('name accepted:',name,not any(x.severity=='error' for x in lint.lint(season)))
    cmd=routes.claude_routes({})[0]['command'].replace('{prompt}','/tmp/prompt.md')
    checked=subprocess.run(['/bin/sh','-n','-c',cmd.split(\"sh -c '\",1)[1][:-1]],capture_output=True,text=True)
    print('generated claude route shell syntax:',checked.returncode,checked.stderr.strip())
    ar=p/'ledger'; ar.mkdir()
    orig_declared=akar._declared; orig_open=builtins.open; orig_replace=os.replace
    barrier=threading.Barrier(2); committed=threading.Event()
    def declared(root):
        result=orig_declared(root); barrier.wait(timeout=5); return result
    def op(path,*args,**kwargs):
        if threading.current_thread().name=='second' and str(path).endswith('.tmp'):
            assert committed.wait(timeout=5)
        return orig_open(path,*args,**kwargs)
    def replace(*args):
        result=orig_replace(*args)
        if threading.current_thread().name=='first': committed.set()
        return result
    results=[]
    def append(body):
        try: results.append((body,str(akar.append_record(ar,'same','title',body))))
        except Exception as e: results.append((body,type(e).__name__))
    with patch.object(akar,'_declared',declared),patch('builtins.open',op),patch.object(os,'replace',replace):
        a=threading.Thread(target=append,args=('first body',),name='first')
        b=threading.Thread(target=append,args=('second body',),name='second')
        a.start();b.start();a.join();b.join()
    print('concurrent ledger append results:',[x[0]+': '+('success' if x[1].endswith('.md') else x[1]) for x in results])
    print('final record body:',akar.find_record(ar,'same').read_text().splitlines()[4])
    # Reused identity: no signal is sent during this reproduction.
    ws=p/'pid';ws.mkdir()
    with patch.object(engine,'_proc_start_ticks',return_value=None),patch.object(engine.os,'killpg') as kill:
        engine._terminate(ws,12345)
        print('termination without identity checks:',[str(x) for x in kill.call_args_list])

/usr/bin/zsh -lc "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 uv run --no-sync python - <<'PY'
import runpy,tempfile,threading,os,signal
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from unittest.mock import patch
from rumpun import engine
f=runpy.run_path('tests/test_rumpun.py')
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rumpun-review-stop-') as td:
    root,season=f['_write_proj'](Path(td),f['SEASON_DUAL'],project_yaml=f['RUMPUN_YAML'].replace('cat {prompt} | true','sleep 30'))
    at_second=threading.Event(); continue_spawn=threading.Event(); second_saved=threading.Event()
    env_original=engine._child_env; save_original=engine._save_state
    calls=0
    def child_env(*args):
        global calls
        calls+=1
        if calls==2:
            at_second.set(); assert continue_spawn.wait(10)
        return env_original(*args)
    def save(root,sid,state,lock_file=None):
        if state.get('status')=='stopped_operator':
            continue_spawn.set(); assert second_saved.wait(10)
        result=save_original(root,sid,state,lock_file)
        if lock_file is not None and 'beta' in state.get('spawned',{}): second_saved.set()
        return result
    with patch.object(engine,'_child_env',child_env),patch.object(engine,'_save_state',save):
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(2) as pool:
            start=pool.submit(engine.start_season,season,root)
            assert at_second.wait(10)
            stopped=pool.submit(engine.stop_season,root,'s1').result(timeout=15)
            result=start.result(timeout=15)
    import json
    beta=json.loads((root/'rimba/s1/beta/state.json').read_text())

 succeeded in 0ms:
transcripts stream into agent.log), so marker families both under-detect and
over-detect. Clean logs carry zero markers because the CLI text output never

### s15 — route rewire proven, stall bug exposed (2026-09-14)

| season | outcome | ships |
|---|---|---|
| s15 | LOSS (stopped_stall at 900s; both writers killed by the stall bug) | the fable route + stream-json is proven live: first non-glm spawns, 6.3MB and 6.8MB event streams carrying Bash tool_use events; the engine stall rule measured runtime instead of progress and is hot-fixed (progress-based, regression test, suite 55/55, record stall-rule-fired-on-runtime) |

The irony is total: the tool-less detector season (s14) suffered a tool-less
boot, and the route-rewire season (s15) proved the rewire while being killed
by its own engine's stall rule. Both writers were mid-build, not stalled:
w1's log grew until 3 seconds before the kill. w1's stream also shows a new
writer-side failure mode — three successive nested-heredoc patch drafts, each
with corruption the writer caught and was repairing when killed. The parser
work moves to s16 with the two real streams as fixtures.

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse Completed
codex
### High

- **H1 — Stop races leave untracked agents running.** [engine.py:196](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:196): `_finalize` reads state and terminates agents before acquiring `state.lock`. Concurrent spawning can therefore add agents after finalization captures its inputs. A controlled reproduction returned `stopped_operator`, omitted `beta` from `spawned`, and left `beta` alive. Concurrent finalizers also returned different terminal decisions. **Fix:** Hold `state.lock` across the terminal-state check, ownership snapshot, termination, and final write. Pass that lock into `_save_state`.

- **H2 — Termination loses the PID-reuse guard.** [engine.py:183](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:183): `_terminate` receives only a PID and signals its process group without checking recorded `proc_start`. Earlier snapshots provide no protection against identity changes before either signal. Finalization also skips crashed leaders, allowing surviving descendants to continue. **Fix:** Pass recorded identity into termination and check ownership before signaling. Retain group ownership through a supervisor or cgroup until every descendant exits.

- **H3 — Agents inherit the caller’s working directory.** [engine.py:299](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:299): `Popen` omits `cwd`, so separate workspace directories provide no execution separation. A `pwd` route returned the repository directory rather than its assigned workspace. Relative output from concurrent agents can overwrite shared files and disappear from workspace-based audits. **Fix:** Resolve workspace paths before spawning and set `cwd=ws`.

- **H4 — Agent names permit directory escapes and harness-state collisions.** [lint.py:243](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/lint.py:243): Lint accepts both `../../outside` and `_season` as agent names. The engine joins these names directly onto the season directory. `_season` makes agent metadata overwrite the harness’s `state.json`. Traversal names place engine writes outside the assigned season. **Fix:** Require nonempty, safe identifier components. Reserve `_season` and reject resolved paths or symlinks outside the season directory.

- **H5 — Startup errors abandon already-spawned agents.** [engine.py:270](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:270): Route checks occur inside the spawn loop, and no cleanup surrounds that loop. A missing second route passed lint, raised `EngineError`, and left the first child running without its watcher. A crash between `Popen` and metadata persistence also leaves an unregistered process that restart can duplicate. **Fix:** Check every spawn input before launching. Persist recoverable spawn intent, then terminate registered children and record startup errors when initialization aborts.

- **H6 — Concurrent appends overwrite immutable evidence.** [akar.py:82](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/akar.py:82): Duplicate checks and publication lack a shared lock. Writers also share a temporary filename and publish with replacing semantics. A controlled reproduction let both same-ID appends succeed, with the second body replacing the first. **Fix:** Lock the complete duplicate-check/publication transaction. Use unique temporary files and publication that refuses existing targets.

- **H7 — Evidence hashes are never checked.** [lint.py:114](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/lint.py:114): `_citation_resolves` ignores the captured digest and accepts any file containing the requested ID as a substring. Both an incorrect hash and a partial record ID passed reproduction checks. Altered or unrelated evidence therefore passes the evolution gate. **Fix:** Resolve the exact declared record ID, recompute its body digest, and compare the cited digest or permitted prefix.

- **H8 — Rejected seasons remain executable.** [evolve.py:240](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/evolve.py:240): Rejection moves YAML into `musim/rejected/`, but startup checks neither rejection records nor canonical season placement. A rejected file passed lint and ran successfully from that directory. Rollback likewise records containment without stopping an active season. **Fix:** Check persisted lifecycle state before apply/start. Coordinate rollback with engine termination before recording containment.

- **H9 — Agent budgets become one shared maximum.** [engine.py:127](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:127): The engine uses the largest agent budget as the season deadline. A one-minute agent alongside a sixty-minute agent therefore receives sixty minutes. The engine also never checks a configured campaign cost cap. **Fix:** Enforce each agent’s deadline independently. Check campaign usage against the configured cap before admitting additional work.

### Medium

- **M1 — Report rendering violates determinism.** [report.py:635](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/report.py:635): Reports call `read_status`, which reads clocks and `/proc` for running seasons. Changing only the mocked clock produced different HTML from unchanged ledger files. Workspace links also follow unsorted `rglob` order at line 594. Campaign links depend on previously rendered reports, making output depend on render history. **Fix:** Render from one persisted snapshot, sort workspace paths, and derive navigation from declared inputs. Hash the exact snapshot bytes used.

- **M2 — Audit invents dead phases for valid artifacts.** [audit.py:138](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/audit.py:138): Artifact discovery recognizes only the fixed `LEDGER_ARTIFACTS` list. A declared `custom.jsonl` present in both audited seasons still produced “0 of 2” and a trim candidate. The denominator also includes seasons before the latest phase existed, plus unfinished seasons. **Fix:** Check each declared output directly. Count only completed seasons that actually declared the corresponding phase and contract.

- **M3 — Harvest verdicts never reach reports or audit.** [harvest.py:61](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/harvest.py:61): Harvest writes its verdict exclusively into an akar record. Reports and audit instead read season-level rows from `rimba/<sid>/verdicts.jsonl`. A successful `WIN` harvest therefore still rendered `INVALID` in reproduction. Audit’s verdict-dependent candidates also miss that decision. **Fix:** Choose one authoritative season-verdict record and make harvest, report, and audit use it consistently.

- **M4 — Live harvest permanently occupies the final harvest ID.** [harvest.py:57](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/harvest.py:57): Harvest only warns when a season remains running, then creates the immutable `<sid>-harvest` record. A later harvest cannot record the actual terminal outcome because that ID already exists. **Fix:** Refuse harvest until the season reaches a terminal state.

- **M5 — Failed agents produce successful command exits.** [engine.py:330](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:330): `TERMINAL` includes `failed` and `crashed`, yet any all-terminal snapshot becomes `completed`. [cli.py:215](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/cli.py:215) then returns zero unconditionally, including budget and stall stops. Shell automation can advance after every agent errors. **Fix:** Distinguish successful completion from unsuccessful termination and map those outcomes to documented nonzero command exits.

- **M6 — Payloads override lane sequence and sender.** [collab.py:53](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/collab.py:53): Payload expansion occurs after harness-assigned fields. `append_event(lane, "actual", {"seq": 99, "from": "forged"})` records the forged values. This defeats dense sequencing and sender attribution despite the writer lock. **Fix:** Reject reserved payload keys or assign authoritative fields after payload expansion.

- **M7 — Lane readers can observe partial appends.** [collab.py:66](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/collab.py:66): Readers acquire no shared lock. Large JSON rows require multiple underlying writes, allowing readers to parse an incomplete final row during a valid append. A crashed writer also leaves a partial tail that subsequent appends extend into permanent corruption. **Fix:** Read under `LOCK_SH` on the same lock file. Check and explicitly recover incomplete tails under `LOCK_EX` before appending.

- **M8 — Rejection and rollback cause season-ID reuse.** [evolve.py:114](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/evolve.py:114): Draft numbering scans only top-level `musim/s*.yaml`. Moving the latest season into `rejected/` makes the next draft reuse its ID. Existing approval records then collide, and terminal engine state prevents the replacement season from starting. **Fix:** Allocate IDs from a persistent high-water mark or every lifecycle record, including rejected seasons.

- **M9 — Malformed input escapes domain error handling.** [lint.py:170](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/lint.py:170): Lint records an invalid methodology, then immediately calls `.get` on it. `methodology: null` therefore raises uncaught `AttributeError`. Other node and ledger consumers similarly assume mappings. [yamlio.py:43](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/yamlio.py:43) also skips lists, allowing nested non-string keys through its advertised check. **Fix:** Check container types before traversal, recurse through sequences, and report path-specific domain errors through the CLI.

- **M10 — Mtime-only activity defeats stall detection.** [engine.py:146](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/engine.py:146): Touching `agent.log` resets progress without changing content or completing work. Repeated noise can therefore prevent stall termination until the budget expires. This contradicts section 13’s explicit exclusion of mtime-only changes and repeated log lines. **Fix:** Track accepted durable progress events or changed artifact content, with a defined grace interval.

- **M11 — Generated login routes contain invalid shell syntax.** [routes.py:186](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/routes.py:186): The generated command retains `--model <model>`. `write_routes` persists that placeholder unchanged, and the engine never substitutes it. Checking the inner shell command with `/bin/sh -n` returns exit 2. **Fix:** Resolve a concrete model before publishing the route and reject unresolved placeholders during preflight.

### Low

- **L1 — Discovery links disagree with generated filenames.** [report.py:454](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/src/rumpun/report.py:454): Page filenames lowercase IDs and replace punctuation, while links use the original ID. Valid IDs containing uppercase letters or underscores therefore produce broken links. Distinct IDs can also normalize to the same filename and overwrite pages. **Fix:** Use one collision-free filename mapping for both links and writes.

- **L2 — The dual-start test depends on scheduler timing.** [tests/test_rumpun.py:465](/mnt/data/work/rumpun/tests/test_rumpun.py:465): Back-to-back `Popen` calls do not guarantee that the second starter reaches the lock before completion. The route exits immediately, so the second process can correctly encounter an already-finished season and exit nonzero. **Fix:** Hold the first starter inside the spawn transaction with an explicit barrier, then release it after checking contention.

### First three fixes

1. **H1 — Finalization locking:** Operator stop currently leaves live, untracked agents.
2. **H4 — Workspace containment:** Accepted names can overwrite harness state or escape season directories.
3. **H6 — Evidence append serialization:** Concurrent writes silently replace evidence that later evolution treats as immutable.
